Done. Here's what was fixed:

- **Problem**: GitHub Actions requires reusable workflows at the top level of `.github/workflows/`, not in subdirectories like `reusable/`.
- **Fix**: Moved all 5 reusable workflows from `.github/workflows/reusable/` to `.github/workflows/` (renamed with `-reusable` suffix) and updated all 8 references across [deploy.yml](.github/workflows/deploy.yml), [pull-request.yml](.github/workflows/pull-request.yml), and [main-validation.yml](.github/workflows/main-validation.yml).

```
     1→name: Pull Request Validation
     2→
     3→on:
     4→  pull_request:
     5→    types: [opened, synchronize, reopened]
     6→    branches:
     7→      - main
     8→      - develop
     9→    paths:
    10→      - 'terraform/**'
    11→      - '.github/workflows/**'
    12→
    13→permissions:
    14→  id-token: write
    15→  contents: read
    16→  pull-requests: write
    17→
    18→jobs:
    19→  detect-changes:
    20→    name: Detect Changed Directories
    21→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    22→    outputs:
    23→      directories: ${{ steps.set-matrix.outputs.directories }}
    24→      has_changes: ${{ steps.set-matrix.outputs.has_changes }}
    25→      has_module_changes: ${{ steps.check-modules.outputs.has_module_changes }}
    26→
    27→    steps:
    28→      - name: Checkout code
    29→        uses: actions/checkout@v4
    30→        with:
    31→          fetch-depth: 0
    32→
    33→      - name: Get changed Terraform directories
    34→        id: changed-files
    35→        run: |
    36→          # Get changed files in PR
    37→          git diff --name-only origin/${{ github.base_ref }}...HEAD > changed_files.txt
    38→
    39→          # Filter for Terraform files
    40→          grep "^terraform/" changed_files.txt | grep "\.tf$" > tf_files.txt || true
    41→
    42→          # Extract unique directories (up to environment level)
    43→          cat tf_files.txt | while read file; do
    44→            # Extract directory path
    45→            dirname "$file"
    46→          done | sort -u > directories.txt
    47→
    48→          echo "Changed directories:"
    49→          cat directories.txt
    50→
    51→      - name: Check for module changes
    52→        id: check-modules
    53→        run: |
    54→          if grep -q "^terraform/modules/" directories.txt; then
    55→            echo "has_module_changes=true" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
    56→            echo "Module changes detected - will run tests"
    57→          else
    58→            echo "has_module_changes=false" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
    59→            echo "No module changes - skipping tests"
    60→          fi
    61→
    62→      - name: Build matrix
    63→        id: set-matrix
    64→        run: |
    65→          # Filter for environment and module directories only
    66→          # Exclude test directories
    67→          grep -E "^terraform/(environments|modules)/" directories.txt | \
    68→            grep -v "/test/" | \
    69→            grep -v "\.md$" > matrix_dirs.txt || echo "" > matrix_dirs.txt
    70→
    71→          if [ -s matrix_dirs.txt ]; then
    72→            # Convert to JSON array
    73→            DIRS=$(jq -R -s -c 'split("\n") | map(select(length > 0))' matrix_dirs.txt)
    74→            echo "directories=$DIRS" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
    75→            echo "has_changes=true" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
    76→            echo "Will validate directories: $DIRS"
    77→          else
    78→            echo "directories=[]" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
    79→            echo "has_changes=false" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
    80→            echo "No Terraform changes to validate"
    81→          fi
    82→
    83→  validate:
    84→    name: Validate
    85→    needs: detect-changes
    86→    if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.has_changes == 'true'
    87→    strategy:
    88→      matrix:
    89→        directory: ${{ fromJson(needs.detect-changes.outputs.directories) }}
    90→      fail-fast: false
    91→    uses: ./.github/workflows/terraform-validate-reusable.yml
    92→    with:
    93→      working_directory: ${{ matrix.directory }}
    94→      terraform_version: '1.5.0'
    95→      enable_tfsec: true
    96→      tfsec_severity: 'MEDIUM'
    97→
    98→  plan:
    99→    name: Plan
   100→    needs: [detect-changes, validate]
   101→    if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.has_changes == 'true'
   102→    strategy:
   103→      matrix:
   104→        directory: ${{ fromJson(needs.detect-changes.outputs.directories) }}
   105→      fail-fast: false
   106→    uses: ./.github/workflows/terraform-plan-reusable.yml
   107→    secrets: inherit
   108→    with:
   109→      working_directory: ${{ matrix.directory }}
   110→      terraform_version: '1.5.0'
   111→      environment: ${{ contains(matrix.directory, '/dev/') && 'dev' || contains(matrix.directory, '/qa/') && 'qa' || contains(matrix.directory, '/prod/') && 'prod' || contains(matrix.directory, '/shared/') && 'shared' || 'lab' }}
   112→      gcp_project_id: ${{ contains(matrix.directory, '/dev/') && 'kd-ix-eur-dev-foundation' || contains(matrix.directory, '/qa/') && 'kd-ix-eur-qa-foundation' || contains(matrix.directory, '/prod/') && 'kd-ix-eur-prod-foundation' || contains(matrix.directory, '/shared/') && 'kd-ix-eur-shr-platform' || 'kd-ix-eur-lab-platform' }}
   113→      backend_bucket: ${{ contains(matrix.directory, '/dev/') && 'kd-tfstate-dev' || contains(matrix.directory, '/qa/') && 'kd-tfstate-qa' || contains(matrix.directory, '/prod/') && 'kd-tfstate-prod' || contains(matrix.directory, '/shared/') && 'kd-tfstate-shared' || 'kd-tfstate-lab' }}
   114→      backend_prefix: ${{ matrix.directory }}/terraform.tfstate
   115→      comment_on_pr: true
   116→      upload_artifact: false
   117→
   118→  fast-tests:
   119→    name: Fast Tests
   120→    needs: [detect-changes, validate]
   121→    if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.has_module_changes == 'true'
   122→    uses: ./.github/workflows/terraform-test-reusable.yml
   123→    secrets: inherit
   124→    with:
   125→      test_suite: 'fast'
   126→      test_timeout: '20m'
   127→      parallel_count: 4
   128→      gcp_project_id: 'kd-ix-eur-lab-platform'
   129→      cleanup_on_failure: true
   130→
   131→  summary:
   132→    name: Validation Summary
   133→    needs: [detect-changes, validate, plan, fast-tests]
   134→    if: always()
   135→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
   136→    steps:
   137→      - name: Generate Summary
   138→        run: |
   139→          echo "## Pull Request Validation Summary" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   140→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   141→
   142→          if [ "${{ needs.detect-changes.outputs.has_changes }}" == "false" ]; then
   143→            echo "ℹ️ No Terraform changes detected" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   144→            exit 0
   145→          fi
   146→
   147→          echo "### Results" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   148→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   149→
   150→          # Validate results
   151→          if [ "${{ needs.validate.result }}" == "success" ]; then
   152→            echo "✅ **Validation:** Passed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   153→          else
   154→            echo "❌ **Validation:** Failed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   155→          fi
   156→
   157→          # Plan results
   158→          if [ "${{ needs.plan.result }}" == "success" ]; then
   159→            echo "✅ **Plan:** Generated successfully" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   160→          else
   161→            echo "❌ **Plan:** Failed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   162→          fi
   163→
   164→          # Test results (if ran)
   165→          if [ "${{ needs.detect-changes.outputs.has_module_changes }}" == "true" ]; then
   166→            if [ "${{ needs.fast-tests.result }}" == "success" ]; then
   167→              echo "✅ **Fast Tests:** Passed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   168→            elif [ "${{ needs.fast-tests.result }}" == "skipped" ]; then
   169→              echo "⏭️ **Fast Tests:** Skipped" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   170→            else
   171→              echo "❌ **Fast Tests:** Failed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   172→            fi
   173→          fi
   174→
   175→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   176→          echo "---" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   177→          echo "**Next Steps:**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   178→          echo "- Review the plan output in PR comments" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   179→          echo "- Address any validation or test failures" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   180→          echo "- Once approved and merged, changes can be deployed using the manual deploy workflow" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   181→
   182→      - name: Check Overall Status
   183→        if: needs.validate.result == 'failure' || needs.plan.result == 'failure' || needs.fast-tests.result == 'failure'
   184→        run: |
   185→          echo "::error::PR validation failed. Please review the errors above."
   186→          exit 1
   187→

But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.
</system-reminder>
```

> AGENT

Found the root cause. The `detect-changes` job outputs `has_changes=true` but `directories=[]` (empty array). This happens because:

1. No terraform files changed (only `.github/workflows/` files), so `directories.txt` is empty
2. The fallback `echo "" > matrix_dirs.txt` writes a newline, making `-s` (file has size > 0) return true
3. So `has_changes=true` with an empty `[]` matrix, which causes the validate job to fail

The fix is in the `set-matrix` step — the empty fallback should truly be empty.

> TOOL


> AGENT

Pushed. Here's the summary:

**Root cause**: In the `set-matrix` step, when no terraform directories are found, the fallback `echo "" > matrix_dirs.txt` writes a newline byte. The `-s` check (file has size > 0) then returns true, setting `has_changes=true` with `directories=[]`. GitHub Actions treats an empty matrix as a failure, causing the Validate job to fail.

**Fix**: Changed `|| echo "" > matrix_dirs.txt` to `|| true` in both [pull-request.yml](.github/workflows/pull-request.yml) and [main-validation.yml](.github/workflows/main-validation.yml). Now when grep finds nothing, `matrix_dirs.txt` stays truly empty (0 bytes), `-s` returns false, and `has_changes` correctly becomes `false`.

> TOOL

> AGENT

Here's the PR description:

---

**Title:** Merge develop to main — Infrastructure restructure & workflow overhaul

**Body:**

```markdown
## Summary

- **Terraform restructured to per-environment layout**: Migrated from monolithic `terraform/root/` and local modules to `environments/{dev,qa,prod}/terraform/`, composing remote modules from `kix-platform-modules`
- **GitHub Actions workflows overhauled**: Replaced monolithic workflow files with reusable workflow pattern (`terraform-{validate,plan,apply,test,drift}-reusable.yml`) and added PR validation, main-branch validation, and deploy workflows with dynamic matrix-based change detection
- **Kubernetes & legacy cleanup**: Removed stale kustomize overlays, backup configs, pod-security manifests, Cloud Armor/GCS/Secret Manager/SSL local modules, and ArgoCD bootstrap files. Helm values and ArgoCD application configs moved under `environments/`
- **Docs & DX**: Added `CLAUDE.md`, archived stale root-level docs, updated README and docs index. Fixed credential wiring to pods

## Key changes

### Infrastructure
- Per-environment Terraform configs (`environments/{dev,qa,prod}/terraform/`) replacing `terraform/root/`
- Removed local Terraform modules in favor of remote `kix-platform-modules`
- Removed `terraform/test/`, `terraform/platform-data-backup/`, and all local module directories

### CI/CD Workflows
- New: `pull-request.yml`, `main-validation.yml`, `deploy.yml`
- New reusable workflows: validate, plan, apply, test, drift
- Removed: `terraform-apply.yaml`, `terraform-plan.yaml`, `terraform-test.yaml`, `health-checks.yaml`
- Fixed empty matrix detection bug in change detection logic

### Kubernetes
- Migrated ArgoCD app configs and Helm values into `environments/{dev,qa,prod}/`
- Removed `kubernetes/kustomize/`, `kubernetes/backup/`, `kubernetes/security/`, `argocd/bootstrap/`

## Test plan

- [x] PR validation workflow passes on develop
- [ ] Verify per-environment Terraform plans generate cleanly
- [ ] Confirm ArgoCD syncs with new environment paths
- [ ] Validate deploy workflow triggers correctly on manual dispatch

🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)
```

> AGENT

The issue is clear. The `detect-changes` job picks up **deleted** `terraform/modules/` directories in the diff (they exist on `main` but were removed on `develop`). The validate job then tries to run in directories that don't exist on the PR branch.

The fix: filter out deleted files from the diff using `--diff-filter=ACMR` (only Added, Copied, Modified, Renamed).

> AGENT

Done. Here's the situation:

**Root cause**: The `git diff --name-only` in `detect-changes` includes **deleted** files. Since `develop` removes `terraform/modules/{cloud-armor,cloud-sql,gcs-buckets,secret-manager,service-accounts,ssl-certificates}`, those directories show up in the diff matrix, but don't exist on the PR branch — causing all 6 validate jobs to fail with "No such file or directory".

**Fix**: Added `--diff-filter=ACMR` to only include Added, Copied, Modified, and Renamed files (excludes Deleted).

**PR #14** created: https://github.com/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/pull/14

Once PR #14 is merged to `develop`, re-running PR #13's checks should pass.

> DEVELOPER

why were these skipped? Pull Request Validation / Fast Tests (pull_request)
Pull Request Validation / Fast Tests (pull_request)Skipped 1 minute ago
Pull Request Validation / Plan (pull_request)
Pull Request Validation / Plan (pull_request)Skipped 1 minute ago
Pull Request Validation / Validate (pull_request)
Pull Request Validation / Validate (pull_request)Skipped 1 minute ago

> AGENT

That's expected behavior. PR #14 only changes `.github/workflows/*.yml` files — no terraform `.tf` files were added or modified.

The `detect-changes` job filters for `^terraform/...\.tf$` files. Since it finds none, `has_changes` is set to `false`, and the three downstream jobs all have conditions gating on that:

- **Validate**: `if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.has_changes == 'true'` → skipped
- **Plan**: `needs: [detect-changes, validate]` + same `has_changes` condition → skipped
- **Fast Tests**: `if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.has_module_changes == 'true'` → skipped

This is correct — there's no terraform to validate on a workflow-only PR. The Validation Summary should still pass since it checks for `result == 'failure'`, and `skipped` is not `failure`.

> AGENT

There's the problem. The terraform files are under `environments/{dev,qa,prod}/terraform/`, but the grep filter on line 40 only looks for `^terraform/`:

```
grep "^terraform/" changed_files.txt
```

It misses `environments/dev/terraform/`, `environments/prod/terraform/`, etc. And the matrix filter on line 67 also only matches `^terraform/(environments|modules)/`.

The detect-changes logic needs to match the new directory structure.

```
    74→            echo "directories=$DIRS" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
    75→            echo "has_changes=true" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
    76→            echo "Will validate directories: $DIRS"
    77→          else

    79→            echo "has_changes=false" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT

    81→          fi
```

> AGENT

Pushed. The issue was that the workflows were written for the old `terraform/` layout but the codebase migrated to `environments/{dev,qa,prod}/terraform/`. Updated in both files:

1. **Path triggers** — added `environments/*/terraform/**` 
2. **File filter grep** — matches both `^terraform/` and `^environments/.../terraform/`
3. **Module changes check** — also matches environment terraform dirs
4. **Matrix builder grep** — same pattern update

PR #14 should pick this up. Once merged to develop, PR #13's checks will detect the environment terraform changes correctly.

```
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Dev Environment - GitHub Actions OIDC for Service Deployments
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Creates Workload Identity Pools and IAM bindings for service repositories
# to deploy via GitHub Actions without service account keys.
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------

provider "google" {
  project = local.gke_project_id
}

#------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# GitHub Actions OIDC Configuration
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------

module "github_actions_oidc" {
  for_each = local.enabled_services

  source = "github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules//terraform/gcp/github-actions-oidc?ref=develop"

  # Project configuration
  project_id  = local.gke_project_id
  environment = local.environment

  # Service identity
  service_name = each.key

  # GitHub repository configuration
  github_org          = local.service_deployments.github_org
  github_repo         = each.value.github_repo
  github_branches     = try(each.value.github_branches, ["main"])
  github_environments = try(each.value.github_environments, [])

  # IAM permissions
  permission_preset = try(each.value.permission_preset, "standard-deployment")
  custom_roles      = try(each.value.custom_roles, [])

  # Cross-project access
  cross_project_access = try(each.value.cross_project_access, {})

  # Labels
  labels = merge(
    local.config.global.common_labels,
    {
      service = each.key
      team    = try(each.value.labels.team, "unknown")
    },
    try(each.value.labels, {})
  )
}
```

```
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Dev Environment - IAM - Local Variables and Data Sources
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------

locals {
  config              = jsondecode(file("${path.module}/../../config.json"))
  environment         = local.config.global.environment
  gke_project_id      = local.config.global.gke_project_id
  service_deployments = local.config.service_deployments

  # Filter enabled services only
  enabled_services = {
    for service_name, service_config in local.service_deployments.services :
    service_name => service_config
    if try(service_config.enabled, false)
  }
}
```

id: toolu_013ERCygs75WTr1grcK4gGyr
```
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# GitHub Actions OIDC Authentication for GCP
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Creates a GCP Service Account with Workload Identity Federation binding to
# GitHub Actions OIDC provider. Enables keyless authentication from GitHub
# workflows to GCP resources.
#
# Security Features:
# - No service account keys created (org policy compliant)
# - Repository-scoped access via attribute conditions
# - Optional branch and environment restrictions
# - Non-authoritative IAM bindings to prevent conflicts
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------

terraform {
  required_version = ">= 1.5.0"

  required_providers {
    google = {
      source  = "hashicorp/google"
      version = ">= 5.0"
    }
  }
}

#------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Service Account
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# GCP Service Account that GitHub Actions will impersonate via OIDC.
# Naming: github-{service-name}-{environment}
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------

resource "google_service_account" "github_actions" {
  project      = var.project_id
  account_id   = "github-${var.service_name}-${var.environment}"
  display_name = "GitHub Actions - ${var.service_name} (${var.environment})"
  description  = "Service account for GitHub Actions deployments of ${var.service_name} in ${var.environment} environment"
}

#------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Workload Identity Pool
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Pool that groups external identity providers (GitHub OIDC in this case).
# One pool per service-environment combination for isolation.
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------

resource "google_iam_workload_identity_pool" "github" {
  project                   = var.project_id
  workload_identity_pool_id = "github-${var.service_name}-${var.environment}"
  display_name              = "GitHub Actions Pool - ${var.service_name} (${var.environment})"
  description               = "Workload Identity Pool for ${var.service_name} GitHub Actions in ${var.environment}"
  disabled                  = false
}

#------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Workload Identity Provider (GitHub OIDC)
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configures GitHub as a trusted OIDC identity provider with security
# restrictions via attribute conditions.
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------

resource "google_iam_workload_identity_pool_provider" "github" {
  project                            = var.project_id
  workload_identity_pool_id          = google_iam_workload_identity_pool.github.workload_identity_pool_id
  workload_identity_pool_provider_id = "github-provider"
  display_name                       = "GitHub OIDC Provider - ${var.service_name}"
  description                        = "GitHub OIDC provider for ${var.github_org}/${var.github_repo}"

  # Map GitHub OIDC token claims to GCP attributes
  attribute_mapping = {
    "google.subject"        = "assertion.sub"
    "attribute.actor"       = "assertion.actor"
    "attribute.repository"  = "assertion.repository"
    "attribute.ref"         = "assertion.ref"
    "attribute.environment" = "assertion.environment"
  }

  # Security condition - restricts which GitHub workflows can authenticate
  attribute_condition = local.attribute_condition

  oidc {
    issuer_uri = "https://token.actions.githubusercontent.com"
  }
}

#------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Attribute Condition Logic (Security Restrictions)
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Builds CEL (Common Expression Language) conditions to restrict access by:
# - Repository (required)
# - Branches (optional - if specified)
# - Environments (optional - if specified)
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------

locals {
  # Build branch filter: assertion.ref == 'refs/heads/main' || assertion.ref == 'refs/heads/develop'
  branch_conditions = length(var.github_branches) > 0 ? [
    for branch in var.github_branches :
    "assertion.ref == 'refs/heads/${branch}'"
  ] : []

  # Build environment filter: assertion.environment == 'dev' || assertion.environment == 'qa'
  env_conditions = length(var.github_environments) > 0 ? [
    for env in var.github_environments :
    "assertion.environment == '${env}'"
  ] : []

  # Combine all conditions with AND logic
  filters = concat(
    # Required: Repository must match
    ["assertion.repository == '${var.github_org}/${var.github_repo}'"],

    # Optional: If branches specified, ref must match one of them
    length(local.branch_conditions) > 0 ? ["(${join(" || ", local.branch_conditions)})"] : [],

    # Optional: If environments specified, environment must match one of them
    length(local.env_conditions) > 0 ? ["(${join(" || ", local.env_conditions)})"] : []
  )

  # Final CEL condition string
  attribute_condition = join(" && ", local.filters)
}

#------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Service Account IAM Binding (GitHub → Service Account)
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Allows GitHub Actions workflows matching the attribute condition to
# impersonate this service account via Workload Identity.
# Uses principalSet to match all tokens from the repository.
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------

resource "google_service_account_iam_member" "workload_identity" {
  service_account_id = google_service_account.github_actions.name
  role               = "roles/iam.workloadIdentityUser"

  # Principal set - all authenticated requests from the GitHub repository
  member = "principalSet://iam.googleapis.com/${google_iam_workload_identity_pool.github.name}/attribute.repository/${var.github_org}/${var.github_repo}"
}

#------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Project IAM Bindings (Service Account → GCP Resources)
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Grants the service account permissions in the project based on the selected
# permission preset or custom roles.
# Uses google_project_iam_member (non-authoritative) to prevent conflicts.
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------

resource "google_project_iam_member" "service_account_roles" {
  for_each = toset(local.final_roles)

  project = var.project_id
  role    = each.value
  member  = "serviceAccount:${google_service_account.github_actions.email}"
}

#------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Cross-Project IAM Bindings (Optional)
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Grants the service account permissions in other GCP projects.
# Useful for services that need to access shared resources (Cloud SQL, GCS, etc.)
#
# Example: Service in kd-ix-eur-dev-gke needs Cloud SQL access in kd-ix-eur-dev-data
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------

locals {
  # Flatten cross_project_access map into list of {project, role} tuples
  # Input: {"project-a" = ["roles/cloudsql.client", "roles/storage.objectViewer"]}
  # Output: [
  #   {project = "project-a", role = "roles/cloudsql.client"},
  #   {project = "project-a", role = "roles/storage.objectViewer"}
  # ]
  cross_project_bindings = flatten([
    for project_id, roles in var.cross_project_access : [
      for role in roles : {
        key     = "${project_id}-${role}"
        project = project_id
        role    = role
      }
    ]
  ])
}

resource "google_project_iam_member" "cross_project_access" {
  for_each = { for binding in local.cross_project_bindings : binding.key => binding }

  project = each.value.project
  role    = each.value.role
  member  = "serviceAccount:${google_service_account.github_actions.email}"
}
```

tool_result
id: toolu_016UfMqyMe3zaw86ug7CWGfE
```
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# GitHub Actions OIDC Module - Outputs
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Outputs provide the configuration needed to set up GitHub Actions workflows.
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------

output "service_account_email" {
  description = "Service account email address for GitHub Actions to impersonate"
  value       = google_service_account.github_actions.email
}

output "service_account_name" {
  description = "Fully qualified service account resource name"
  value       = google_service_account.github_actions.name
}

output "service_account_id" {
  description = "Service account ID (account_id@project.iam.gserviceaccount.com)"
  value       = google_service_account.github_actions.account_id
}

output "workload_identity_provider" {
  description = "Workload Identity Provider resource name (full path)"
  value       = google_iam_workload_identity_pool_provider.github.name
}

output "workload_identity_provider_id" {
  description = "Full Workload Identity Provider ID for use in GitHub Actions workflow configuration"
  value       = google_iam_workload_identity_pool_provider.github.id
}

output "workload_identity_pool_id" {
  description = "Workload Identity Pool resource name"
  value       = google_iam_workload_identity_pool.github.name
}

output "workload_identity_pool_name" {
  description = "Workload Identity Pool ID (short name)"
  value       = google_iam_workload_identity_pool.github.workload_identity_pool_id
}

output "github_actions_config" {
  description = "Configuration object for GitHub Actions workflow. Use these values in your .github/workflows/*.yml files."
  value = {
    workload_identity_provider = google_iam_workload_identity_pool_provider.github.name
    service_account            = google_service_account.github_actions.email
    project_id                 = var.project_id
    repository                 = "${var.github_org}/${var.github_repo}"
  }
}

output "attribute_condition" {
  description = "CEL attribute condition applied to the Workload Identity Provider (for debugging)"
  value       = local.attribute_condition
}

output "granted_roles" {
  description = "List of IAM roles granted to the service account in the primary project"
  value       = local.final_roles
}

output "cross_project_access" {
  description = "Map of cross-project IAM bindings"
  value       = var.cross_project_access
}

output "permission_preset" {
  description = "Permission preset used for this configuration"
  value       = var.permission_preset
}
```

> DEVELOPER

does this handle different workload identity and SAs per environment?

> AGENT

No, it doesn't. Right now it uses a single pair of secrets (`GCP_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER` and `GCP_SERVICE_ACCOUNT`) for all environments. That won't work when you need dev, qa, and prod each with their own OIDC pool and service account.

The reusable workflows (`terraform-plan.yml`, `terraform-apply.yml`, etc.) already receive an `environment` input from the callers. The cleanest fix is to use GitHub environment-scoped secrets — set the same secret names under each GitHub environment (dev, qa, prod), and add an `environment` declaration to the reusable workflow jobs so they pull the right secrets automatically.

Want me to update the workflows to use environment-scoped secrets?

> DEVELOPER

ok thanks, thats good

> AGENT

Sounds good. To summarize where things stand:

- **`feature/gcp-oidc-auth`** — pushed with single-secret OIDC auth, ready for PR to develop
- **`feature/github-workflow-updates`** — has all the workflow replacements from the platform repo
- **PR #13** (`develop` → `main`) — will need the workflow changes merged to develop first
- **Secrets to set** — `GCP_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER` and `GCP_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` from the OIDC module outputs in `kix-platform-infrastructure`

When you're ready to support multi-environment, we can switch to environment-scoped secrets. Just let me know.

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01HhHsYN8Bogh95HaBpj576Z
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/.github/workflows/validate-and-plan.yml",
  "old_string": "          # Get changed files in PR\n          git diff --name-only origin/${{ github.base_ref }}...HEAD > changed_files.txt",
  "new_string": "          # Get changed files in PR (fallback to develop for workflow_dispatch)\n          BASE_REF=\"${{ github.base_ref || 'develop' }}\"\n          git diff --name-only origin/${BASE_REF}...HEAD > changed_files.txt"
}
```

```
     1→name: Auto Validate and Plan
     2→
     3→# Triggers on pull requests to validate Terraform changes before merge
     4→# Runs validation, security scanning, planning, and generates summary per environment
     5→# For manual validation/planning, use the "Manual Validate and Plan" workflow instead
     6→on:
     7→  pull_request:
     8→    types: [opened, synchronize, reopened]
     9→    branches:
    10→      - main
    11→      - develop
    12→    paths:
    13→      - 'terraform/**'
    14→      - 'environments/**'
    15→      - '.github/workflows/**'
    16→
    17→permissions:
    18→  id-token: write
    19→  contents: read
    20→  pull-requests: write
    21→
    22→jobs:
    23→  detect-changes:
    24→    name: Detect Changed Directories
    25→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    26→    outputs:
    27→      matrix: ${{ steps.set-matrix.outputs.matrix }}
    28→      has_changes: ${{ steps.set-matrix.outputs.has_changes }}
    29→      has_module_changes: ${{ steps.check-modules.outputs.has_module_changes }}
    30→
    31→    steps:
    32→      - name: Checkout code
    33→        uses: actions/checkout@v4
    34→        with:
    35→          fetch-depth: 0
    36→
    37→      - name: Get changed Terraform directories
    38→        id: changed-files
    39→        run: |
    40→          # Get changed files in PR (fallback to develop for workflow_dispatch)
    41→          BASE_REF="${{ github.base_ref || 'develop' }}"
    42→          git diff --name-only origin/${BASE_REF}...HEAD > changed_files.txt
    43→
    44→          # Filter for Terraform files
    45→          grep -E "^(environments|modules|terraform)/" changed_files.txt | grep "\.tf$" > tf_files.txt || true
    46→
    47→          # Extract unique directories (up to environment level)
    48→          cat tf_files.txt | while read file; do
    49→            # Extract directory path
    50→            dirname "$file"
    51→          done | sort -u > directories.txt
    52→
    53→          echo "Changed directories:"
    54→          cat directories.txt
    55→
    56→      - name: Check for module changes
    57→        id: check-modules
    58→        run: |
    59→          if grep -qE "^(modules|terraform/modules)/" directories.txt; then
    60→            echo "has_module_changes=true" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
    61→            echo "Module changes detected - will run tests"
    62→          else
    63→            echo "has_module_changes=false" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
    64→            echo "No module changes - skipping tests"
    65→          fi
    66→
    67→      - name: Build matrix
    68→        id: set-matrix
    69→        run: |
    70→          # Filter for environment and module directories only
    71→          # Exclude automated testing directories (managed by weekly-quality.yml only)
    72→          grep -E "^(environments|modules|terraform/environments|terraform/modules)/" directories.txt | \
    73→            grep -v "/automated-testing/" | \
    74→            grep -v "/test/" | \
    75→            grep -v "\.md$" > matrix_dirs.txt || > matrix_dirs.txt
    76→
    77→          if [ -s matrix_dirs.txt ]; then
    78→            # Build matrix with directory and backend_prefix using jq
    79→            # Strip "environments/{env}/" prefix to match backend.tf pattern
    80→            # For bootstrap, strip just "environments/" to get "bootstrap"
    81→            MATRIX=$(while IFS= read -r dir; do
    82→              backend_prefix=$(echo "$dir" | sed -E 's#^environments/(dev|qa|prod|shared|lab)/##; s#^environments/##')
    83→              echo "$dir|$backend_prefix"
    84→            done < matrix_dirs.txt | jq -R -s -c '
    85→              split("\n") |
    86→              map(select(length > 0)) |
    87→              map(split("|") | {directory: .[0], backend_prefix: .[1]})
    88→            ')
    89→
    90→            echo "matrix=$MATRIX" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
    91→            echo "has_changes=true" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
    92→            echo "Will validate matrix: $MATRIX"
    93→          else
    94→            echo "matrix=[]" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
    95→            echo "has_changes=false" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
    96→            echo "No Terraform changes to validate"
    97→          fi
    98→
    99→  validate:
   100→    name: Validate
   101→    needs: detect-changes
   102→    if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.has_changes == 'true' && needs.detect-changes.outputs.matrix != '[]'
   103→    strategy:
   104→      matrix:
   105→        include: ${{ fromJson(needs.detect-changes.outputs.matrix) }}
   106→      fail-fast: false
   107→    uses: ./.github/workflows/terraform-validate.yml
   108→    secrets: inherit
   109→    with:
   110→      working_directory: ${{ matrix.directory }}
   111→      terraform_version: '1.7.4'
   112→      enable_tfsec: true
   113→      tfsec_severity: 'MEDIUM'
   114→
   115→  plan:
   116→    name: Plan
   117→    needs: [detect-changes, validate]
   118→    if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.has_changes == 'true' && needs.detect-changes.outputs.matrix != '[]'
   119→    strategy:
   120→      matrix:
   121→        include: ${{ fromJson(needs.detect-changes.outputs.matrix) }}
   122→      fail-fast: false
   123→    uses: ./.github/workflows/terraform-plan.yml
   124→    secrets: inherit
   125→    with:
   126→      working_directory: ${{ matrix.directory }}
   127→      terraform_version: '1.7.4'
   128→      environment: ${{ startsWith(matrix.directory, 'environments/bootstrap') && 'bootstrap' || contains(matrix.directory, '/dev/') && 'dev' || contains(matrix.directory, '/qa/') && 'qa' || contains(matrix.directory, '/prod/') && 'prod' || contains(matrix.directory, '/shared/') && 'shared' || 'lab' }}
   129→      gcp_project_id: ${{ startsWith(matrix.directory, 'environments/bootstrap') && 'kd-ix-eur-shr-bootstrap' || contains(matrix.directory, '/dev/') && 'kd-ix-eur-dev-foundation' || contains(matrix.directory, '/qa/') && 'kd-ix-eur-qa-foundation' || contains(matrix.directory, '/prod/') && 'kd-ix-eur-prod-foundation' || contains(matrix.directory, '/shared/') && 'kd-ix-eur-shr-platform' || 'kd-ix-eur-lab-platform' }}
   130→      backend_bucket: ${{ startsWith(matrix.directory, 'environments/bootstrap') && 'kd-tfstate-bootstrap' || contains(matrix.directory, '/dev/') && 'kd-tfstate-dev' || contains(matrix.directory, '/qa/') && 'kd-tfstate-qa' || contains(matrix.directory, '/prod/') && 'kd-tfstate-prod' || contains(matrix.directory, '/shared/') && 'kd-tfstate-shared' || 'kd-tfstate-lab' }}
   131→      # Backend prefix computed in matrix (stripped "environments/{env}/" to match backend.tf)
   132→      backend_prefix: ${{ matrix.backend_prefix }}
   133→      comment_on_pr: true
   134→      upload_artifact: false
   135→
   136→  aggregate-plan-results:
   137→    name: Aggregate Plan Results
   138→    needs: [detect-changes, plan]
   139→    if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.has_changes == 'true' && needs.detect-changes.outputs.matrix != '[]'
   140→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
   141→    outputs:
   142→      plan_table: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.plan_table }}
   143→      success_count: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.success_count }}
   144→      failure_count: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.failure_count }}
   145→      total_add: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.total_add }}
   146→      total_change: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.total_change }}
   147→      total_destroy: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.total_destroy }}
   148→
   149→    steps:
   150→      - name: Download all plan statistics
   151→        uses: actions/download-artifact@v4
   152→        continue-on-error: true
   153→        with:
   154→          pattern: plan-stats-*
   155→          path: plan-results/
   156→          merge-multiple: true
   157→
   158→      - name: Aggregate plan results
   159→        id: aggregate
   160→        run: |
   161→          # Initialize counters
   162→          SUCCESS_COUNT=0
   163→          FAILURE_COUNT=0
   164→          TOTAL_ADD=0
   165→          TOTAL_CHANGE=0
   166→          TOTAL_DESTROY=0
   167→
   168→          # Build table header WITH STATUS COLUMN
   169→          PLAN_TABLE="| Environment | Directory | Status | Add | Change | Destroy |\n"
   170→          PLAN_TABLE="${PLAN_TABLE}|-------------|-----------|--------|-----|--------|---------|"
   171→
   172→          # Process all plan stats JSON files (plan_stats_*.json pattern)
   173→          if [ -d "plan-results" ]; then
   174→            for statfile in plan-results/plan_stats_*.json; do
   175→              if [ -f "$statfile" ]; then
   176→                DIRECTORY=$(jq -r '.directory' "$statfile")
   177→                ENVIRONMENT=$(jq -r '.environment' "$statfile")
   178→                ADD=$(jq -r '.add' "$statfile")
   179→                CHANGE=$(jq -r '.change' "$statfile")
   180→                DESTROY=$(jq -r '.destroy' "$statfile")
   181→                STATUS=$(jq -r '.status // "success"' "$statfile")
   182→
   183→                # Handle empty directory
   184→                if [ -z "$DIRECTORY" ] || [ "$DIRECTORY" == "null" ]; then
   185→                  DIRECTORY="(unknown)"
   186→                fi
   187→
   188→                if [ "$STATUS" == "success" ]; then
   189→                  STATUS_EMOJI="✅"
   190→                  SUCCESS_COUNT=$((SUCCESS_COUNT + 1))
   191→                  TOTAL_ADD=$((TOTAL_ADD + ADD))
   192→                  TOTAL_CHANGE=$((TOTAL_CHANGE + CHANGE))
   193→                  TOTAL_DESTROY=$((TOTAL_DESTROY + DESTROY))
   194→                else
   195→                  STATUS_EMOJI="❌"
   196→                  FAILURE_COUNT=$((FAILURE_COUNT + 1))
   197→                  ADD="-"
   198→                  CHANGE="-"
   199→                  DESTROY="-"
   200→                fi
   201→
   202→                PLAN_TABLE="${PLAN_TABLE}\n| ${ENVIRONMENT} | \`${DIRECTORY}\` | ${STATUS_EMOJI} | ${ADD} | ${CHANGE} | ${DESTROY} |"
   203→              fi
   204→            done
   205→
   206→            # ADD TOTALS ROW
   207→            TOTAL_DIRS=$((SUCCESS_COUNT + FAILURE_COUNT))
   208→            PLAN_TABLE="${PLAN_TABLE}\n| **TOTALS** | | **${SUCCESS_COUNT}/${TOTAL_DIRS} succeeded** | **${TOTAL_ADD}** | **${TOTAL_CHANGE}** | **${TOTAL_DESTROY}** |"
   209→          else
   210→            PLAN_TABLE="${PLAN_TABLE}\n| - | *No plan results found* | - | - | - | - |"
   211→          fi
   212→
   213→          # Save outputs
   214→          {
   215→            echo 'plan_table<<EOF'
   216→            echo -e "$PLAN_TABLE"
   217→            echo 'EOF'
   218→          } >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   219→
   220→          echo "success_count=${SUCCESS_COUNT}" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   221→          echo "failure_count=${FAILURE_COUNT}" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   222→          echo "total_add=${TOTAL_ADD}" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   223→          echo "total_change=${TOTAL_CHANGE}" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   224→          echo "total_destroy=${TOTAL_DESTROY}" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   225→
   226→  aggregate-security-findings:
   227→    name: Aggregate Security Findings
   228→    needs: [detect-changes, validate]
   229→    if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.has_changes == 'true' && needs.detect-changes.outputs.matrix != '[]'
   230→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
   231→    outputs:
   232→      has_critical: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.has_critical }}
   233→      has_high: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.has_high }}
   234→      critical_count: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.critical_count }}
   235→      high_count: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.high_count }}
   236→      medium_count: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.medium_count }}
   237→      low_count: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.low_count }}
   238→      total_count: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.total_count }}
   239→
   240→    steps:
   241→      - name: Download all SARIF artifacts
   242→        uses: actions/download-artifact@v4
   243→        continue-on-error: true
   244→        with:
   245→          pattern: trivy-results-*
   246→          path: sarif-results/
   247→          merge-multiple: true
   248→
   249→      - name: Aggregate findings from all directories
   250→        id: aggregate
   251→        run: |
   252→          CRITICAL=0
   253→          HIGH=0
   254→          MEDIUM=0
   255→          LOW=0
   256→
   257→          # Find all text result files (more reliable than SARIF)
   258→          if [ -d "sarif-results" ]; then
   259→            for txtfile in sarif-results/*.txt; do
   260→              if [ -f "$txtfile" ]; then
   261→                echo "Processing $txtfile..."
   262→
   263→                # Parse counts from text output (same logic as terraform-validate.yml)
   264→                CRITICAL_IN_FILE=$(grep -oP 'CRITICAL:\s*\K\d+' "$txtfile" | awk '{s+=$1} END {print s+0}')
   265→                HIGH_IN_FILE=$(grep -oP 'HIGH:\s*\K\d+' "$txtfile" | awk '{s+=$1} END {print s+0}')
   266→                MEDIUM_IN_FILE=$(grep -oP 'MEDIUM:\s*\K\d+' "$txtfile" | awk '{s+=$1} END {print s+0}')
   267→                LOW_IN_FILE=$(grep -oP 'LOW:\s*\K\d+' "$txtfile" | awk '{s+=$1} END {print s+0}')
   268→
   269→                CRITICAL=$((CRITICAL + CRITICAL_IN_FILE))
   270→                HIGH=$((HIGH + HIGH_IN_FILE))
   271→                MEDIUM=$((MEDIUM + MEDIUM_IN_FILE))
   272→                LOW=$((LOW + LOW_IN_FILE))
   273→
   274→                echo "  CRITICAL: $CRITICAL_IN_FILE, HIGH: $HIGH_IN_FILE, MEDIUM: $MEDIUM_IN_FILE, LOW: $LOW_IN_FILE"
   275→              fi
   276→            done
   277→          else
   278→            echo "No Trivy results found"
   279→          fi
   280→
   281→          TOTAL=$((CRITICAL + HIGH + MEDIUM + LOW))
   282→
   283→          echo "critical_count=$CRITICAL" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   284→          echo "high_count=$HIGH" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   285→          echo "medium_count=$MEDIUM" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   286→          echo "low_count=$LOW" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   287→          echo "total_count=$TOTAL" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   288→
   289→          # Set boolean flags for easier condition checking
   290→          if [ "$CRITICAL" -gt 0 ]; then
   291→            echo "has_critical=true" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   292→          else
   293→            echo "has_critical=false" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   294→          fi
   295→
   296→          if [ "$HIGH" -gt 0 ]; then
   297→            echo "has_high=true" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   298→          else
   299→            echo "has_high=false" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   300→          fi
   301→
   302→  fast-tests:
   303→    name: Fast Tests
   304→    needs: [detect-changes, validate]
   305→    if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.has_module_changes == 'true'
   306→    uses: ./.github/workflows/terraform-test.yml
   307→    secrets: inherit
   308→    with:
   309→      test_suite: 'fast'
   310→      test_timeout: '20m'
   311→      parallel_count: 4
   312→      gcp_project_id: 'kd-ix-eur-lab-platform'
   313→      cleanup_on_failure: true
   314→
   315→  security-review:
   316→    name: Security Review Gate
   317→    needs: [detect-changes, validate, plan, aggregate-plan-results, aggregate-security-findings]
   318→    # Run if security findings exist, regardless of whether plan succeeded or failed
   319→    if: |
   320→      !cancelled() &&
   321→      needs.detect-changes.outputs.has_changes == 'true' &&
   322→      needs.detect-changes.outputs.matrix != '[]' &&
   323→      needs.aggregate-security-findings.result == 'success' &&
   324→      (needs.aggregate-security-findings.outputs.has_critical == 'true' || needs.aggregate-security-findings.outputs.has_high == 'true')
   325→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
   326→    environment:
   327→      name: security-review
   328→    steps:
   329→      - name: Security Review Required
   330→        run: |
   331→          echo "## 🚨 Security Review Required" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   332→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   333→          echo "Trivy detected HIGH or CRITICAL severity security findings." >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   334→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   335→          echo "**Findings:**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   336→          echo "- 🔴 CRITICAL: ${{ needs.validate.outputs.tfsec_critical_count }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   337→          echo "- 🟠 HIGH: ${{ needs.validate.outputs.tfsec_high_count }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   338→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   339→          echo "**Action Required:**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   340→          echo "1. Review the security findings in the 'Validate' job above" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   341→          echo "2. Assess the risk and impact of the findings" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   342→          echo "3. Approve this workflow to continue OR reject to block" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   343→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   344→          echo "⚠️ **Note:** This is a manual security gate. LOW/MEDIUM findings are informational only." >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   345→
   346→  summary:
   347→    name: Validation Summary
   348→    needs: [detect-changes, validate, plan, aggregate-plan-results, aggregate-security-findings, fast-tests, security-review]
   349→    if: always()
   350→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
   351→    steps:
   352→      - name: Generate Summary
   353→        run: |
   354→          echo "## 📊 Validation and Plan Summary" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   355→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   356→
   357→          if [ "${{ needs.detect-changes.outputs.has_changes }}" == "false" ]; then
   358→            echo "ℹ️ No Terraform changes detected" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   359→            exit 0
   360→          fi
   361→
   362→          # Get aggregated counts
   363→          SUCCESS_COUNT="${{ needs.aggregate-plan-results.outputs.success_count || 0 }}"
   364→          FAILURE_COUNT="${{ needs.aggregate-plan-results.outputs.failure_count || 0 }}"
   365→          TOTAL_ADD="${{ needs.aggregate-plan-results.outputs.total_add || 0 }}"
   366→          TOTAL_CHANGE="${{ needs.aggregate-plan-results.outputs.total_change || 0 }}"
   367→          TOTAL_DESTROY="${{ needs.aggregate-plan-results.outputs.total_destroy || 0 }}"
   368→          CRITICAL="${{ needs.aggregate-security-findings.outputs.critical_count || 0 }}"
   369→          HIGH="${{ needs.aggregate-security-findings.outputs.high_count || 0 }}"
   370→          MEDIUM="${{ needs.aggregate-security-findings.outputs.medium_count || 0 }}"
   371→
   372→          # Determine overall status
   373→          TOTAL_ENVS=$((SUCCESS_COUNT + FAILURE_COUNT))
   374→          if [ "$FAILURE_COUNT" -gt 0 ]; then
   375→            OVERALL_STATUS="❌ Failed"
   376→          elif [ "$CRITICAL" -gt 0 ] || [ "$HIGH" -gt 0 ]; then
   377→            OVERALL_STATUS="⚠️ Review Required"
   378→          else
   379→            OVERALL_STATUS="✅ Ready"
   380→          fi

   398→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY

   404→            if [ "${{ needs.security-review.result }}" == "success" ]; then

   406→            elif [ "${{ needs.security-review.result }}" == "failure" ]; then

   427→            fi

   429→          fi

   432→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   433→
   434→          # Next Steps

   436→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   437→
   438→          if [ "$FAILURE_COUNT" -gt 0 ]; then

   440→          elif [ "$CRITICAL" -gt 0 ] || [ "$HIGH" -gt 0 ]; then

   443→            else

   445→            fi

   448→          fi
   449→
   450→      - name: Check Overall Status
   451→        if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.has_changes == 'true'
   452→        run: |

   456→             [ "${{ needs.fast-tests.result }}" == "failure" ] || \
```

Now I have the full picture. Key differences from the platform repo:

- **Paths**: `environments/{env}/terraform/` (not `environments/{env}/gcp/{scope}/`)
- **Terraform version**: `>= 1.5.0` (not `1.7.4`)
- **Backend prefix**: always `services/crewai/resources` (not computed from directory path)
- **Backend buckets**: `kd-tfstate-{env}`
- **GCP project**: `kd-ix-eur-dev-gke` (from OIDC setup, not `kd-ix-eur-dev-foundation`)
- **No modules, no bootstrap/shared/lab environments**
- **No fast-tests** (no test directory)

    - main\n      - develop\n    paths:\n      - 'environments/*/terraform/**'\n      - '.github/workflows/**'\n\npermissions:\n  id-token: write\n  contents: read\n  pull-requests: write\n\njobs:\n  detect-changes:\n    name: Detect Changed Directories\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    outputs:\n      matrix: ${{ steps.set-matrix.outputs.matrix }}\n      has_changes: ${{ steps.set-matrix.outputs.has_changes }}\n\n    steps:\n      - name: Checkout code\n        uses: actions/checkout@v4\n        with:\n          fetch-depth: 0\n\n      - name: Get changed Terraform directories\n        id: changed-files\n        run: |\n          # Get changed files in PR (exclude deletions)\n          BASE_REF=\"${{ github.base_ref || 'develop' }}\"\n          git diff --diff-filter=ACMR --name-only origin/${BASE_REF}...HEAD > changed_files.txt\n\n          # Filter for Terraform files in environments\n          grep -E \"^environments/[^/]+/terraform/\" changed_files.txt | grep \"\\.tf$\" > tf_files.txt || true\n\n          # Extract unique directories\n          cat tf_files.txt | while read file; do\n            dirname \"$file\"\n          done | sort -u > directories.txt\n\n          echo \"Changed directories:\"\n          cat directories.txt\n\n      - name: Build matrix\n        id: set-matrix\n        run: |\n          if [ -s directories.txt ]; then\n            # Build matrix with directory, environment, and backend config\n            MATRIX=$(while IFS= read -r dir; do\n              env=$(echo \"$dir\" | cut -d'/' -f2)\n              echo \"$dir|$env\"\n            done < directories.txt | jq -R -s -c '\n              split(\"\\n\") |\n              map(select(length > 0)) |\n              map(split(\"|\") | {directory: .[0], environment: .[1]})\n            ')\n\n            echo \"matrix=$MATRIX\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n            echo \"has_changes=true\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n            echo \"Will validate matrix: $MATRIX\"\n          else\n            echo \"matrix=[]\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n            echo \"has_changes=false\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n            echo \"No Terraform changes to validate\"\n          fi\n\n  validate:\n    name: Validate\n    needs: detect-changes\n    if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.has_changes == 'true' && needs.detect-changes.outputs.matrix != '[]'\n    strategy:\n      matrix:\n        include: ${{ fromJson(needs.detect-changes.outputs.matrix) }}\n      fail-fast: false\n    uses: ./.github/workflows/terraform-validate.yml\n    secrets: inherit\n    with:\n      working_directory: ${{ matrix.directory }}\n      terraform_version: '1.5.0'\n      enable_tfsec: true\n      tfsec_severity: 'MEDIUM'\n\n  plan:\n    name: Plan\n    needs: [detect-changes, validate]\n    if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.has_changes == 'true' && needs.detect-changes.outputs.matrix != '[]'\n    strategy:\n      matrix:\n        include: ${{ fromJson(needs.detect-changes.outputs.matrix) }}\n      fail-fast: false\n    uses: ./.github/workflows/terraform-plan.yml\n    secrets: inherit\n    with:\n      working_directory: ${{ matrix.directory }}\n      terraform_version: '1.5.0'\n      environment: ${{ matrix.environment }}\n      gcp_project_id: ${{ matrix.environment == 'dev' && 'kd-ix-eur-dev-gke' || matrix.environment == 'qa' && 'kd-ix-eur-qa-gke' || 'kd-ix-eur-prod-gke' }}\n      backend_bucket: ${{ matrix.environment == 'dev' && 'kd-tfstate-dev' || matrix.environment == 'qa' && 'kd-tfstate-qa' || 'kd-tfstate-prod' }}\n      backend_prefix: services/crewai/resources\n      comment_on_pr: true\n      upload_artifact: false\n\n  aggregate-security-findings:\n    name: Aggregate Security Findings\n    needs: [detect-changes, validate]\n    if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.has_changes == 'true' && needs.detect-changes.outputs.matrix != '[]'\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    outputs:\n      has_critical: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.has_critical }}\n      has_high: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.has_high }}\n      critical_count: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.critical_count }}\n      high_count: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.high_count }}\n      medium_count: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.medium_count }}\n      low_count: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.low_count }}\n      total_count: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.total_count }}\n\n    steps:\n      - name: Download all SARIF artifacts\n        uses: actions/download-artifact@v4\n        continue-on-error: true\n        with:\n          pattern: trivy-results-*\n          path: sarif-results/\n          merge-multiple: true\n\n      - name: Aggregate findings from all directories\n        id: aggregate\n        run: |\n          CRITICAL=0\n          HIGH=0\n          MEDIUM=0\n          LOW=0\n\n          if [ -d \"sarif-results\" ]; then\n            for txtfile in sarif-results/*.txt; do\n              if [ -f \"$txtfile\" ]; then\n                echo \"Processing $txtfile...\"\n                CRITICAL_IN_FILE=$(grep -oP 'CRITICAL:\\s*\\K\\d+' \"$txtfile\" | awk '{s+=$1} END {print s+0}')\n                HIGH_IN_FILE=$(grep -oP 'HIGH:\\s*\\K\\d+' \"$txtfile\" | awk '{s+=$1} END {print s+0}')\n                MEDIUM_IN_FILE=$(grep -oP 'MEDIUM:\\s*\\K\\d+' \"$txtfile\" | awk '{s+=$1} END {print s+0}')\n                LOW_IN_FILE=$(grep -oP 'LOW:\\s*\\K\\d+' \"$txtfile\" | awk '{s+=$1} END {print s+0}')\n\n                CRITICAL=$((CRITICAL + CRITICAL_IN_FILE))\n                HIGH=$((HIGH + HIGH_IN_FILE))\n                MEDIUM=$((MEDIUM + MEDIUM_IN_FILE))\n                LOW=$((LOW + LOW_IN_FILE))\n\n                echo \"  CRITICAL: $CRITICAL_IN_FILE, HIGH: $HIGH_IN_FILE, MEDIUM: $MEDIUM_IN_FILE, LOW: $LOW_IN_FILE\"\n              fi\n            done\n          else\n            echo \"No Trivy results found\"\n          fi\n\n          TOTAL=$((CRITICAL + HIGH + MEDIUM + LOW))\n\n          echo \"critical_count=$CRITICAL\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          echo \"high_count=$HIGH\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          echo \"medium_count=$MEDIUM\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          echo \"low_count=$LOW\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          echo \"total_count=$TOTAL\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n\n          if [ \"$CRITICAL\" -gt 0 ]; then\n            echo \"has_critical=true\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          else\n            echo \"has_critical=false\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          fi\n\n          if [ \"$HIGH\" -gt 0 ]; then\n            echo \"has_high=true\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          else\n            echo \"has_high=false\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          fi\n\n  summary:\n    name: Validation Summary\n    needs: [detect-changes, validate, plan, aggregate-security-findings]\n    if: always()\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    steps:\n      - name: Generate Summary\n        run: |\n          echo \"## Validation and Plan Summary\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n\n          if [ \"${{ needs.detect-changes.outputs.has_changes }}\" == \"false\" ]; then\n            echo \"No Terraform changes detected\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            exit 0\n          fi\n\n          echo \"### Results\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n\n          # Validate results\n          if [ \"${{ needs.validate.result }}\" == \"success\" ]; then\n            echo \"- Validation: Passed\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          elif [ \"${{ needs.validate.result }}\" == \"skipped\" ]; then\n            echo \"- Validation: Skipped\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          else\n            echo \"- Validation: Failed\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          fi\n\n          # Plan results\n          if [ \"${{ needs.plan.result }}\" == \"success\" ]; then\n            echo \"- Plan: Generated successfully\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          elif [ \"${{ needs.plan.result }}\" == \"skipped\" ]; then\n            echo \"- Plan: Skipped\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          else\n            echo \"- Plan: Failed\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          fi\n\n          # Security findings\n          CRITICAL=\"${{ needs.aggregate-security-findings.outputs.critical_count || 0 }}\"\n          HIGH=\"${{ needs.aggregate-security-findings.outputs.high_count || 0 }}\"\n          MEDIUM=\"${{ needs.aggregate-security-findings.outputs.medium_count || 0 }}\"\n          if [ \"$CRITICAL\" -gt 0 ] || [ \"$HIGH\" -gt 0 ]; then\n            echo \"- Security: CRITICAL=$CRITICAL HIGH=$HIGH MEDIUM=$MEDIUM\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          elif [ \"$MEDIUM\" -gt 0 ]; then\n            echo \"- Security: MEDIUM=$MEDIUM (informational)\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          else\n            echo \"- Security: No findings\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          fi\n\n          echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"---\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"**Next Steps:**\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"- Review the plan output in PR comments\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"- Address any validation or security failures\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"- Once approved and merged, deploy via the 'Validate, Plan and Apply' workflow\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n\n      - name: Check Overall Status\n        if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.has_changes == 'true'\n        run: |\n          if [ \"${{ needs.validate.result }}\" == \"failure\" ] || \\\n             [ \"${{ needs.plan.result }}\" == \"failure\" ]; then\n            echo \"::error::Validation failed. Please review the errors above.\"\n            exit 1\n          fi\n"
```json
}
```

```
   113→    if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.has_changes == 'true' && needs.detect-changes.outputs.matrix != '[]'
   114→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
   115→    outputs:
   116→      has_critical: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.has_critical }}
   117→      has_high: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.has_high }}
   118→      critical_count: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.critical_count }}
   119→      high_count: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.high_count }}
   120→      medium_count: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.medium_count }}
   121→      low_count: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.low_count }}
   122→      total_count: ${{ steps.aggregate.outputs.total_count }}
   123→
   124→    steps:
   125→      - name: Download all SARIF artifacts
   126→        uses: actions/download-artifact@v4
   127→        continue-on-error: true
   128→        with:
   129→          pattern: trivy-results-*
   130→          path: sarif-results/
   131→          merge-multiple: true
   132→
   133→      - name: Aggregate findings from all directories
   134→        id: aggregate
   135→        run: |
   136→          CRITICAL=0
   137→          HIGH=0
   138→          MEDIUM=0
   139→          LOW=0
   140→
   141→          if [ -d "sarif-results" ]; then
   142→            for txtfile in sarif-results/*.txt; do
   143→              if [ -f "$txtfile" ]; then
   144→                echo "Processing $txtfile..."
   145→                CRITICAL_IN_FILE=$(grep -oP 'CRITICAL:\s*\K\d+' "$txtfile" | awk '{s+=$1} END {print s+0}')
   146→                HIGH_IN_FILE=$(grep -oP 'HIGH:\s*\K\d+' "$txtfile" | awk '{s+=$1} END {print s+0}')
   147→                MEDIUM_IN_FILE=$(grep -oP 'MEDIUM:\s*\K\d+' "$txtfile" | awk '{s+=$1} END {print s+0}')
   148→                LOW_IN_FILE=$(grep -oP 'LOW:\s*\K\d+' "$txtfile" | awk '{s+=$1} END {print s+0}')
   149→
   150→                CRITICAL=$((CRITICAL + CRITICAL_IN_FILE))
   151→                HIGH=$((HIGH + HIGH_IN_FILE))
   152→                MEDIUM=$((MEDIUM + MEDIUM_IN_FILE))
   153→                LOW=$((LOW + LOW_IN_FILE))
   154→
   155→                echo "  CRITICAL: $CRITICAL_IN_FILE, HIGH: $HIGH_IN_FILE, MEDIUM: $MEDIUM_IN_FILE, LOW: $LOW_IN_FILE"
   156→              fi
   157→            done
   158→          else
   159→            echo "No Trivy results found"
   160→          fi
   161→
   162→          TOTAL=$((CRITICAL + HIGH + MEDIUM + LOW))
   163→
   164→          echo "critical_count=$CRITICAL" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   165→          echo "high_count=$HIGH" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   166→          echo "medium_count=$MEDIUM" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   167→          echo "low_count=$LOW" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   168→          echo "total_count=$TOTAL" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   169→
   170→          if [ "$CRITICAL" -gt 0 ]; then
   171→            echo "has_critical=true" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   172→          else
   173→            echo "has_critical=false" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   174→          fi
   175→
   176→          if [ "$HIGH" -gt 0 ]; then
   177→            echo "has_high=true" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   178→          else
   179→            echo "has_high=false" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   180→          fi
   181→
   182→  summary:
   183→    name: Validation Summary
   184→    needs: [detect-changes, validate, plan, aggregate-security-findings]
   185→    if: always()
   186→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
   187→    steps:
   188→      - name: Generate Summary
   189→        run: |
   190→          echo "## Validation and Plan Summary" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   191→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   192→
   193→          if [ "${{ needs.detect-changes.outputs.has_changes }}" == "false" ]; then
   194→            echo "No Terraform changes detected" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   195→            exit 0
   196→          fi
   197→
   198→          echo "### Results" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   199→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   200→
   201→          # Validate results
   202→          if [ "${{ needs.validate.result }}" == "success" ]; then
   203→            echo "- Validation: Passed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   204→          elif [ "${{ needs.validate.result }}" == "skipped" ]; then
   205→            echo "- Validation: Skipped" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   206→          else
   207→            echo "- Validation: Failed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   208→          fi
   209→
   210→          # Plan results
   211→          if [ "${{ needs.plan.result }}" == "success" ]; then
   212→            echo "- Plan: Generated successfully" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   213→          elif [ "${{ needs.plan.result }}" == "skipped" ]; then
   214→            echo "- Plan: Skipped" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   215→          else
   216→            echo "- Plan: Failed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   217→          fi
   218→
   219→          # Security findings
   220→          CRITICAL="${{ needs.aggregate-security-findings.outputs.critical_count || 0 }}"
   221→          HIGH="${{ needs.aggregate-security-findings.outputs.high_count || 0 }}"
   222→          MEDIUM="${{ needs.aggregate-security-findings.outputs.medium_count || 0 }}"
   223→          if [ "$CRITICAL" -gt 0 ] || [ "$HIGH" -gt 0 ]; then
   224→            echo "- Security: CRITICAL=$CRITICAL HIGH=$HIGH MEDIUM=$MEDIUM" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   225→          elif [ "$MEDIUM" -gt 0 ]; then
   226→            echo "- Security: MEDIUM=$MEDIUM (informational)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   227→          else
   228→            echo "- Security: No findings" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   229→          fi
   230→
   231→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   232→          echo "---" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   233→          echo "**Next Steps:**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   234→          echo "- Review the plan output in PR comments" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   235→          echo "- Address any validation or security failures" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   236→          echo "- Once approved and merged, deploy via the 'Validate, Plan and Apply' workflow" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   237→
   238→      - name: Check Overall Status
   239→        if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.has_changes == 'true'
   240→        run: |
   241→          if [ "${{ needs.validate.result }}" == "failure" ] || \
   242→             [ "${{ needs.plan.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
   243→            echo "::error::Validation failed. Please review the errors above."
   244→            exit 1
   245→          fi
   246→
```

```
    49→          # Define allowed branch patterns

    52→          elif [[ "$BRANCH" == "main" ]]; then
    53→            BRANCH_TYPE="main"

    55→            BRANCH_TYPE="develop"
    56→          else

    59→            exit 1
    60→          fi
    61→
    62→          # Validate branch-environment combination
    63→          case "$BRANCH_TYPE" in
    64→            tag)
    65→              # Version tags can ONLY deploy to prod
    66→              if [[ "$ENV" != "prod" ]]; then
    67→                echo "::error::Version tags (v*) can only deploy to production"
    68→                echo "::error::Current: $BRANCH → $ENV"
    69→                echo "::error::Allowed: v* → prod"
    70→                exit 1
    71→              fi
    72→              ;;
    73→            main)
    74→              # Main branch can deploy to dev, qa, bootstrap, shared (NOT prod)
    75→              if [[ "$ENV" != "dev" && "$ENV" != "qa" && "$ENV" != "bootstrap" && "$ENV" != "shared" ]]; then

   243→  validate:

   258→    secrets: inherit
   259→    with:

   268→      upload_artifact: true
   269→
   270→  review-gate:
   271→    name: Review Plan
   272→    needs: [pre-flight, validate, plan]
   273→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
   274→    environment:
   275→      name: ${{ inputs.environment }}-approval
   276→    steps:
   277→      - name: Plan Review Summary
   278→        run: |
   279→          echo "## 📋 Plan Review Required" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   280→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY

   282→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   283→          echo "**Review the plan output in the previous job before approving.**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   284→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   285→          echo "- **Environment:** ${{ inputs.environment }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   286→          echo "- **Directory:** \`${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.working_directory }}\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   287→          echo "- **Reviewer:** @${{ github.actor }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   288→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   289→          echo "### Next Steps" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   290→          echo "1. Review the plan output from the 'Plan Changes' job above" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   291→          echo "2. Verify all changes are expected and correct" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   292→          echo "3. If satisfied, approve this workflow run to proceed with apply" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   293→          echo "4. If issues found, reject and investigate" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   294→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   295→          echo "⚠️ **This is a manual approval gate. The apply will not proceed until approved.**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   296→
   297→  apply:
   298→    name: Apply Changes
   299→    needs: [pre-flight, review-gate]
   300→    uses: ./.github/workflows/terraform-apply.yml
   301→    secrets: inherit
   302→    with:
   303→      working_directory: ${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.working_directory }}
   304→      terraform_version: '1.7.4'
   305→      environment: ${{ inputs.environment }}
   306→      gcp_project_id: ${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.gcp_project_id }}
   307→      backend_bucket: ${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.backend_bucket }}
   308→      # Backend prefix computed in pre-flight job (stripped "environments/{env}/" to match backend.tf)
   309→      backend_prefix: ${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.backend_prefix }}
   310→
   311→  summary:
   312→    name: Deployment Summary
   313→    needs: [pre-flight, validate, plan, review-gate, apply]
   314→    if: always()
   315→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
   316→    steps:
   317→      - name: Generate Summary
   318→        run: |
   319→          echo "## 🚀 Terraform Deployment Summary" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   320→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   321→
   322→          # Overall Status
   323→          OVERALL_STATUS="✅ Deployed"
   324→          if [ "${{ needs.apply.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
   325→            OVERALL_STATUS="❌ Failed"
   326→          elif [ "${{ needs.review-gate.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
   327→            OVERALL_STATUS="⏸️ Cancelled"
   328→          elif [ "${{ needs.apply.result }}" == "skipped" ]; then
   329→            OVERALL_STATUS="⏭️ Skipped"
   330→          fi
   331→
   332→          echo "### Overall Status: $OVERALL_STATUS" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   333→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   334→
   335→          # Deployment Details
   336→          echo "**Environment:** ${{ inputs.environment }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   337→          echo "**Scope:** ${{ inputs.scope }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   338→          echo "**Directory:** \`${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.working_directory }}\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   339→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   340→
   341→          # Plan Changes (from plan job outputs)
   342→          ADD="${{ needs.plan.outputs.add_count || '0' }}"
   343→          CHANGE="${{ needs.plan.outputs.change_count || '0' }}"
   344→          DESTROY="${{ needs.plan.outputs.destroy_count || '0' }}"
   345→
   346→          echo "**Changes Applied:**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   347→          if [ "$ADD" != "0" ] || [ "$CHANGE" != "0" ] || [ "$DESTROY" != "0" ]; then
   348→            echo "- $ADD to add, $CHANGE to change, $DESTROY to destroy" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   349→          else
   350→            echo "- No infrastructure changes" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   351→          fi
   352→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   353→
   354→          # Job Results
   355→          echo "**Job Results:**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   356→          if [ "${{ needs.validate.result }}" == "success" ]; then
   357→            echo "- ✅ Validation: Passed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   358→          else
   359→            echo "- ❌ Validation: Failed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   360→          fi
   361→
   362→          if [ "${{ needs.plan.result }}" == "success" ]; then
   363→            echo "- ✅ Plan: Generated successfully" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   364→          else
   365→            echo "- ❌ Plan: Failed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   366→          fi
   367→
   368→          if [ "${{ needs.review-gate.result }}" == "success" ]; then
   369→            echo "- ✅ Review: Approved" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   370→          elif [ "${{ needs.review-gate.result }}" == "skipped" ]; then
   371→            echo "- ⏭️ Review: Skipped (previous step failed)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   372→          else
   373→            echo "- ❌ Review: Rejected or failed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   374→          fi
   375→
   376→          if [ "${{ needs.apply.result }}" == "success" ]; then
   377→            echo "- ✅ Apply: Completed successfully" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   378→          elif [ "${{ needs.apply.result }}" == "skipped" ]; then
   379→            echo "- ⏭️ Apply: Skipped" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   380→          else
   381→            echo "- ❌ Apply: Failed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   382→          fi
   383→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   384→
   385→          echo "---" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   386→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   387→
   388→          # Next Steps (contextual)
   389→          echo "### Next Steps" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   390→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   391→
   392→          if [ "${{ needs.apply.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
   393→            echo "❌ **Deployment failed** - Review error logs in apply job above" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   394→          elif [ "${{ needs.review-gate.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
   395→            echo "⏸️ **Deployment cancelled** - Plan was not approved during review" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   396→          elif [ "${{ needs.apply.result }}" == "skipped" ]; then
   397→            echo "⏭️ **Deployment skipped** - Check previous job failures" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   398→          else
   399→            echo "✅ **Deployment complete** - Infrastructure updated for ${{ inputs.environment }} environment" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   400→          fi
   401→
   402→      - name: Check Overall Status
   403→        if: needs.apply.result == 'failure'
   404→        run: |
   405→          echo "::error::Deployment failed. Check the apply job logs for details."
   406→          exit 1
   407→
```
