    25→          - platform
    26→          - data
    27→          - iam
    28→          - kong
    29→          - gateway
    30→          - litellm
    31→          - argocd
    32→
    33→permissions:
    34→  id-token: write
    35→  contents: read
    36→  issues: write
    37→  pull-requests: write
    38→
    39→jobs:
    40→  check-branch:
    41→    name: Check Branch and Environment
    42→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    43→    steps:
    44→      - name: Validate branch-environment combination
    45→        run: |
    46→          BRANCH="${{ github.ref_name }}"
    47→          ENV="${{ inputs.environment }}"
    48→
    49→          # Define allowed branch patterns
    50→          if [[ "$BRANCH" =~ ^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$ ]]; then
    51→            BRANCH_TYPE="tag"
    52→          elif [[ "$BRANCH" == "main" ]]; then
    53→            BRANCH_TYPE="main"
    54→          elif [[ "$BRANCH" == "develop" ]]; then
    55→            BRANCH_TYPE="develop"
    56→          else
    57→            echo "::error::Deployments not allowed from branch: $BRANCH"
    58→            echo "::error::Allowed branches: main, develop, v* tags"
    59→            exit 1
    60→          fi
    61→
    62→          # Validate branch-environment combination
    63→          case "$BRANCH_TYPE" in
    64→            tag)
    65→              # Version tags can ONLY deploy to prod
    66→              if [[ "$ENV" != "prod" ]]; then
    67→                echo "::error::Version tags (v*) can only deploy to production"
    68→                echo "::error::Current: $BRANCH → $ENV"
    69→                echo "::error::Allowed: v* → prod"
    70→                exit 1
    71→              fi
    72→              ;;
    73→            main)
    74→              # Main branch can deploy to dev, qa, bootstrap, shared (NOT prod)
    75→              if [[ "$ENV" != "dev" && "$ENV" != "qa" && "$ENV" != "bootstrap" && "$ENV" != "shared" ]]; then
    76→                echo "::error::Main branch cannot deploy to: $ENV"
    77→                echo "::error::Current: $BRANCH → $ENV"
    78→                echo "::error::Allowed: main → dev, qa, bootstrap, shared"
    79→                echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
    80→                echo "To deploy to production:" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
    81→                echo "1. Create a version tag: git tag -a v1.2.3 -m 'Release 1.2.3'" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
    82→                echo "2. Push the tag: git push origin v1.2.3" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
    83→                echo "3. Run this workflow from the v1.2.3 tag" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
    84→                exit 1
    85→              fi
    86→              ;;
    87→            develop)
    88→              # Develop branch can ONLY deploy to dev environment
    89→              if [[ "$ENV" != "dev" ]]; then
    90→                echo "::error::Develop branch can only deploy to dev environment"
    91→                echo "::error::Current: $BRANCH → $ENV"
    92→                echo "::error::Allowed: develop → dev"
    93→                exit 1
    94→              fi
    95→              ;;
    96→          esac
    97→
    98→          echo "✅ Branch-environment check passed: $BRANCH → $ENV"
    99→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   100→          echo "## 🔒 Access Control" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   101→          echo "- **Branch:** $BRANCH ($BRANCH_TYPE)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   102→          echo "- **Environment:** $ENV ✅" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   103→          echo "- **Status:** Authorized" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   104→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   105→
   106→  pre-flight:
   107→    name: Pre-flight Checks
   108→    needs: check-branch
   109→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
   110→    outputs:
   111→      working_directory: ${{ steps.directory-mapping.outputs.working_directory }}
   112→      gcp_project_id: ${{ steps.directory-mapping.outputs.gcp_project_id }}
   113→      backend_bucket: ${{ steps.directory-mapping.outputs.backend_bucket }}
   114→      backend_prefix: ${{ steps.directory-mapping.outputs.backend_prefix }}
   115→      continue: ${{ steps.checks.outputs.continue }}
   116→
   117→    steps:
   118→      - name: Checkout code
   119→        uses: actions/checkout@v4
   120→
   121→      - name: Validate deployment request
   122→        run: |
   123→          echo "## Deployment Request" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   124→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   125→          echo "- **Environment:** ${{ inputs.environment }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   126→          echo "- **Scope:** ${{ inputs.scope }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   127→          echo "- **Triggered by:** @${{ github.actor }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   128→          echo "- **Branch:** ${{ github.ref_name }} ✅" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   129→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   130→          echo "> **Note:** Branch-environment access control enforced (see Access Control section above)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   131→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   132→
   133→      - name: Validate environment-scope combination
   134→        run: |
   135→          ENV="${{ inputs.environment }}"
   136→          SCOPE="${{ inputs.scope }}"
   137→
   138→          # Bootstrap environment must use bootstrap scope
   139→          if [ "$ENV" == "bootstrap" ] && [ "$SCOPE" != "bootstrap" ]; then
   140→            echo "::error::Bootstrap environment only supports 'bootstrap' scope"
   141→            echo "::error::Current: environment=$ENV, scope=$SCOPE"
   142→            echo "::error::Use scope=bootstrap when deploying to bootstrap environment"
   143→            exit 1
   144→          fi
   145→
   146→          # Other environments cannot use bootstrap scope
   147→          if [ "$ENV" != "bootstrap" ] && [ "$SCOPE" == "bootstrap" ]; then
   148→            echo "::error::Bootstrap scope can only be used with bootstrap environment"
   149→            echo "::error::Current: environment=$ENV, scope=$SCOPE"
   150→            echo "::error::Bootstrap scope is reserved for the bootstrap environment"
   151→            exit 1
   152→          fi
   153→
   154→          echo "✅ Environment-scope combination valid: $ENV → $SCOPE"
   155→
   156→      - name: Check change window (Production only)
   157→        if: inputs.environment == 'prod'
   158→        run: |
   159→          # Get current UTC time
   160→          HOUR=$(date -u +%H)
   161→          DAY=$(date -u +%u)  # 1=Monday, 7=Sunday
   162→
   163→          echo "**Change Window Check**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   164→          echo "- Current UTC time: $(date -u)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   165→          echo "- Day of week: $(date -u +%A)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   166→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   167→
   168→          # Prod changes only allowed Tue-Thu 20:00-23:00 UTC
   169→          if [[ $DAY -lt 2 || $DAY -gt 4 ]]; then
   170→            echo "::error::Production deployments only allowed Tuesday-Thursday"
   171→            echo "❌ **Outside change window:** Production deployments only allowed Tuesday-Thursday" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   172→            exit 1
   173→          fi
   174→
   175→          if [[ $HOUR -lt 20 || $HOUR -gt 23 ]]; then
   176→            echo "::error::Production deployments only allowed 20:00-23:00 UTC"
   177→            echo "❌ **Outside change window:** Production deployments only allowed 20:00-23:00 UTC" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   178→            exit 1
   179→          fi
   180→
   181→          echo "✅ **Within approved change window**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   182→
   183→      - name: Map scope to directory
   184→        id: directory-mapping
   185→        run: |
   186→          ENV="${{ inputs.environment }}"
   187→          SCOPE="${{ inputs.scope }}"
   188→
   189→          # Determine working directory based on environment and scope
   190→          if [ "$SCOPE" == "bootstrap" ]; then
   191→            WORK_DIR="environments/bootstrap"
   192→            PROJECT_ID="kd-ix-eur-shr-bootstrap"
   193→            BUCKET="kd-tfstate-bootstrap"
   194→          elif [ "$SCOPE" == "network" ]; then
   195→            WORK_DIR="environments/$ENV/gcp/network"
   196→            PROJECT_ID="kd-ix-eur-$ENV-network"
   197→            BUCKET="kd-tfstate-$ENV"
   198→          elif [ "$SCOPE" == "security" ]; then
   199→            WORK_DIR="environments/$ENV/gcp/security"
   200→            PROJECT_ID="kd-ix-eur-$ENV-security"
   201→            BUCKET="kd-tfstate-$ENV"
   202→          elif [ "$SCOPE" == "gke" ]; then
   203→            WORK_DIR="environments/$ENV/gcp/gke"
   204→            PROJECT_ID="kd-ix-eur-$ENV-gke"
   205→            BUCKET="kd-tfstate-$ENV"
   206→          elif [ "$SCOPE" == "data" ]; then
   207→            WORK_DIR="environments/$ENV/gcp/data"
   208→            PROJECT_ID="kd-ix-eur-$ENV-platform-data"
   209→            BUCKET="kd-tfstate-$ENV"
   210→          elif [ "$SCOPE" == "iam" ]; then
   211→            WORK_DIR="environments/$ENV/gcp/iam"
   212→            PROJECT_ID="kd-ix-eur-$ENV-gke"
   213→            BUCKET="kd-tfstate-$ENV"
   214→          elif [[ "$SCOPE" == "platform" || "$SCOPE" == "kong" || "$SCOPE" == "gateway" || "$SCOPE" == "litellm" || "$SCOPE" == "argocd" ]]; then
   215→            WORK_DIR="environments/$ENV/kubernetes/$SCOPE"
   216→            PROJECT_ID="kd-ix-eur-$ENV-gke"
   217→            BUCKET="kd-tfstate-$ENV"
   218→          else
   219→            echo "::error::Unknown scope: $SCOPE"
   220→            exit 1
   221→          fi
   222→
   223→          # Compute backend prefix by stripping "environments/{env}/" prefix
   224→          # This matches the backend.tf convention: "gcp/network" not "environments/dev/gcp/network"
   225→          BACKEND_PREFIX=$(echo "$WORK_DIR" | sed -E 's#^environments/(dev|qa|prod|shared|lab)/##; s#^environments/##')
   226→
   227→          echo "working_directory=$WORK_DIR" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   228→          echo "gcp_project_id=$PROJECT_ID" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   229→          echo "backend_bucket=$BUCKET" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   230→          echo "backend_prefix=$BACKEND_PREFIX" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   231→
   232→          echo "**Deployment Target**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   233→          echo "- Directory: \`$WORK_DIR\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   234→          echo "- GCP Project: \`$PROJECT_ID\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   235→          echo "- State Bucket: \`gs://$BUCKET\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   236→          echo "- Backend Prefix: \`$BACKEND_PREFIX\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   237→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   238→
   239→      - name: Checks complete
   240→        id: checks
   241→        run: echo "continue=true" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   242→
   243→  validate:
   244→    name: Validate Terraform
   245→    needs: pre-flight
   246→    uses: ./.github/workflows/terraform-validate.yml
   247→    secrets: inherit
   248→    with:
   249→      working_directory: ${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.working_directory }}
   250→      terraform_version: '1.7.4'
   251→      enable_tfsec: true
   252→      tfsec_severity: 'MEDIUM'
   253→
   254→  plan:
   255→    name: Plan Changes
   256→    needs: [pre-flight, validate]
   257→    uses: ./.github/workflows/terraform-plan.yml
   258→    secrets: inherit
   259→    with:
   260→      working_directory: ${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.working_directory }}
   261→      terraform_version: '1.7.4'
   262→      environment: ${{ inputs.environment }}
   263→      gcp_project_id: ${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.gcp_project_id }}
   264→      backend_bucket: ${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.backend_bucket }}
   265→      # Backend prefix computed in pre-flight job (stripped "environments/{env}/" to match backend.tf)
   266→      backend_prefix: ${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.backend_prefix }}
   267→      comment_on_pr: false
   268→      upload_artifact: true
   269→
   270→  review-gate:
   271→    name: Review Plan
   272→    needs: [pre-flight, validate, plan]
   273→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
   274→    environment:
   275→      name: ${{ inputs.environment }}-approval
   276→    steps:
   277→      - name: Plan Review Summary
   278→        run: |
   279→          echo "## 📋 Plan Review Required" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   280→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   281→          echo "The terraform plan has been generated successfully." >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   282→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   283→          echo "**Review the plan output in the previous job before approving.**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   284→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   285→          echo "- **Environment:** ${{ inputs.environment }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   286→          echo "- **Directory:** \`${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.working_directory }}\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   287→          echo "- **Reviewer:** @${{ github.actor }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   288→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   289→          echo "### Next Steps" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   290→          echo "1. Review the plan output from the 'Plan Changes' job above" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   291→          echo "2. Verify all changes are expected and correct" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   292→          echo "3. If satisfied, approve this workflow run to proceed with apply" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   293→          echo "4. If issues found, reject and investigate" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   294→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   295→          echo "⚠️ **This is a manual approval gate. The apply will not proceed until approved.**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   296→
   297→  apply:
   298→    name: Apply Changes
   299→    needs: [pre-flight, review-gate]
   300→    uses: ./.github/workflows/terraform-apply.yml
   301→    secrets: inherit
   302→    with:
   303→      working_directory: ${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.working_directory }}
   304→      terraform_version: '1.7.4'
   305→      environment: ${{ inputs.environment }}
   306→      gcp_project_id: ${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.gcp_project_id }}
   307→      backend_bucket: ${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.backend_bucket }}
   308→      # Backend prefix computed in pre-flight job (stripped "environments/{env}/" to match backend.tf)
   309→      backend_prefix: ${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.backend_prefix }}
   310→
   311→  summary:
   312→    name: Deployment Summary
   313→    needs: [pre-flight, validate, plan, review-gate, apply]
   314→    if: always()
   315→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
   316→    steps:
   317→      - name: Generate Summary
   318→        run: |
   319→          echo "## 🚀 Terraform Deployment Summary" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   320→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   321→
   322→          # Overall Status
   323→          OVERALL_STATUS="✅ Deployed"
   324→          if [ "${{ needs.apply.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
   325→            OVERALL_STATUS="❌ Failed"
   326→          elif [ "${{ needs.review-gate.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
   327→            OVERALL_STATUS="⏸️ Cancelled"
   328→          elif [ "${{ needs.apply.result }}" == "skipped" ]; then
   329→            OVERALL_STATUS="⏭️ Skipped"
   330→          fi
   331→
   332→          echo "### Overall Status: $OVERALL_STATUS" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   333→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   334→
   335→          # Deployment Details
   336→          echo "**Environment:** ${{ inputs.environment }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   337→          echo "**Scope:** ${{ inputs.scope }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   338→          echo "**Directory:** \`${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.working_directory }}\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   339→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   340→
   341→          # Plan Changes (from plan job outputs)
   342→          ADD="${{ needs.plan.outputs.add_count || '0' }}"
   343→          CHANGE="${{ needs.plan.outputs.change_count || '0' }}"
   344→          DESTROY="${{ needs.plan.outputs.destroy_count || '0' }}"
   345→
   346→          echo "**Changes Applied:**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   347→          if [ "$ADD" != "0" ] || [ "$CHANGE" != "0" ] || [ "$DESTROY" != "0" ]; then
   348→            echo "- $ADD to add, $CHANGE to change, $DESTROY to destroy" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   349→          else
   350→            echo "- No infrastructure changes" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   351→          fi
   352→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   353→
   354→          # Job Results
   355→          echo "**Job Results:**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   356→          if [ "${{ needs.validate.result }}" == "success" ]; then
   357→            echo "- ✅ Validation: Passed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   358→          else
   359→            echo "- ❌ Validation: Failed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   360→          fi
   361→
   362→          if [ "${{ needs.plan.result }}" == "success" ]; then
   363→            echo "- ✅ Plan: Generated successfully" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   364→          else
   365→            echo "- ❌ Plan: Failed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   366→          fi
   367→
   368→          if [ "${{ needs.review-gate.result }}" == "success" ]; then
   369→            echo "- ✅ Review: Approved" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   370→          elif [ "${{ needs.review-gate.result }}" == "skipped" ]; then
   371→            echo "- ⏭️ Review: Skipped (previous step failed)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   372→          else
   373→            echo "- ❌ Review: Rejected or failed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   374→          fi
   375→
   376→          if [ "${{ needs.apply.result }}" == "success" ]; then
   377→            echo "- ✅ Apply: Completed successfully" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   378→          elif [ "${{ needs.apply.result }}" == "skipped" ]; then
   379→            echo "- ⏭️ Apply: Skipped" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   380→          else
   381→            echo "- ❌ Apply: Failed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   382→          fi
   383→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   384→
   385→          echo "---" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   386→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   387→
   388→          # Next Steps (contextual)
   389→          echo "### Next Steps" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   390→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   391→
   392→          if [ "${{ needs.apply.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
   393→            echo "❌ **Deployment failed** - Review error logs in apply job above" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   394→          elif [ "${{ needs.review-gate.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
   395→            echo "⏸️ **Deployment cancelled** - Plan was not approved during review" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   396→          elif [ "${{ needs.apply.result }}" == "skipped" ]; then
   397→            echo "⏭️ **Deployment skipped** - Check previous job failures" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   398→          else
   399→            echo "✅ **Deployment complete** - Infrastructure updated for ${{ inputs.environment }} environment" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   400→          fi
   401→
   402→      - name: Check Overall Status
   403→        if: needs.apply.result == 'failure'
   404→        run: |
   405→          echo "::error::Deployment failed. Check the apply job logs for details."
   406→          exit 1
   407→

But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.
</system-reminder>

     1→name: Nightly Quality Checks
     2→
     3→on:
     4→  schedule:
     5→    # Run daily at 02:00 UTC (off-peak hours)
     6→    - cron: '0 2 * * *'
     7→  workflow_dispatch:
     8→    inputs:
     9→      skip_validation:
    10→        description: 'Skip validation checks'
    11→        required: false
    12→        type: boolean
    13→        default: false
    14→      skip_drift:
    15→        description: 'Skip drift detection'
    16→        required: false
    17→        type: boolean
    18→        default: false
    19→
    20→permissions:
    21→  id-token: write
    22→  contents: read
    23→  issues: write
    24→  pull-requests: write
    25→
    26→jobs:
    27→  # Validate develop branch can deploy to dev environment
    28→  validate-develop-dev:
    29→    name: Validate develop → dev (${{ matrix.scope }})
    30→    if: ${{ !inputs.skip_validation }}
    31→    strategy:
    32→      matrix:
    33→        scope:
    34→          - network
    35→          - security
    36→          - gke
    37→          - data
    38→          - iam
    39→          - platform
    40→      fail-fast: false
    41→    uses: ./.github/workflows/manual-validate-plan.yml
    42→    secrets: inherit
    43→    with:
    44→      environment: 'dev'
    45→      scope: ${{ matrix.scope }}
    46→      ref: 'develop'
    47→
    48→  # Validate main branch can deploy to qa environment
    49→  validate-main-qa:
    50→    name: Validate main → qa (${{ matrix.scope }})
    51→    if: ${{ !inputs.skip_validation }}
    52→    strategy:
    53→      matrix:
    54→        scope:
    55→          - network
    56→          - security
    57→          - gke
    58→          - data
    59→          - iam
    60→          - platform
    61→      fail-fast: false
    62→    uses: ./.github/workflows/manual-validate-plan.yml
    63→    secrets: inherit
    64→    with:
    65→      environment: 'qa'
    66→      scope: ${{ matrix.scope }}
    67→      ref: 'main'
    68→
    69→  # Validate main branch can deploy to shared infrastructure
    70→  validate-main-shared:
    71→    name: Validate main → shared (${{ matrix.scope }})
    72→    if: ${{ !inputs.skip_validation }}
    73→    strategy:
    74→      matrix:
    75→        scope:
    76→          - network
    77→          - security
    78→          - gke
    79→          - iam
    80→          - platform
    81→          - argocd
    82→      fail-fast: false
    83→    uses: ./.github/workflows/manual-validate-plan.yml
    84→    secrets: inherit
    85→    with:
    86→      environment: 'shared'
    87→      scope: ${{ matrix.scope }}
    88→      ref: 'main'
    89→
    90→  drift-detection-dev:
    91→    name: Drift Detection (Dev)
    92→    if: ${{ !inputs.skip_drift }}
    93→    strategy:
    94→      matrix:
    95→        scope:
    96→          - { dir: 'environments/dev/gcp/network', bucket: 'kd-tfstate-dev', project: 'kd-ix-eur-dev-network' }
    97→          - { dir: 'environments/dev/gcp/security', bucket: 'kd-tfstate-dev', project: 'kd-ix-eur-dev-security' }
    98→          - { dir: 'environments/dev/gcp/gke', bucket: 'kd-tfstate-dev', project: 'kd-ix-eur-dev-gke' }
    99→          - { dir: 'environments/dev/kubernetes/platform', bucket: 'kd-tfstate-dev', project: 'kd-ix-eur-dev-gke' }
   100→      fail-fast: false
   101→    uses: ./.github/workflows/terraform-drift.yml
   102→    secrets: inherit
   103→    with:
   104→      working_directory: ${{ matrix.scope.dir }}
   105→      environment: 'dev'
   106→      gcp_project_id: ${{ matrix.scope.project }}
   107→      backend_bucket: ${{ matrix.scope.bucket }}
   108→      backend_prefix: ${{ matrix.scope.dir }}/terraform.tfstate
   109→      notify_on_drift: true
   110→      notification_channel: 'github'
   111→
   112→  drift-detection-qa:
   113→    name: Drift Detection (QA)
   114→    if: ${{ !inputs.skip_drift }}
   115→    strategy:
   116→      matrix:
   117→        scope:
   118→          - { dir: 'environments/qa/gcp/network', bucket: 'kd-tfstate-qa', project: 'kd-ix-eur-qa-network' }
   119→          - { dir: 'environments/qa/gcp/security', bucket: 'kd-tfstate-qa', project: 'kd-ix-eur-qa-security' }
   120→          - { dir: 'environments/qa/gcp/gke', bucket: 'kd-tfstate-qa', project: 'kd-ix-eur-qa-gke' }
   121→          - { dir: 'environments/qa/kubernetes/platform', bucket: 'kd-tfstate-qa', project: 'kd-ix-eur-qa-gke' }
   122→      fail-fast: false
   123→    uses: ./.github/workflows/terraform-drift.yml
   124→    secrets: inherit
   125→    with:
   126→      working_directory: ${{ matrix.scope.dir }}
   127→      environment: 'qa'
   128→      gcp_project_id: ${{ matrix.scope.project }}
   129→      backend_bucket: ${{ matrix.scope.bucket }}
   130→      backend_prefix: ${{ matrix.scope.dir }}/terraform.tfstate
   131→      notify_on_drift: true
   132→      notification_channel: 'github'
   133→
   134→  drift-detection-prod:
   135→    name: Drift Detection (Prod)
   136→    if: ${{ !inputs.skip_drift }}
   137→    strategy:
   138→      matrix:
   139→        scope:
   140→          - { dir: 'environments/prod/gcp/network', bucket: 'kd-tfstate-prod', project: 'kd-ix-eur-prod-network' }
   141→          - { dir: 'environments/prod/gcp/security', bucket: 'kd-tfstate-prod', project: 'kd-ix-eur-prod-security' }
   142→          - { dir: 'environments/prod/gcp/gke', bucket: 'kd-tfstate-prod', project: 'kd-ix-eur-prod-gke' }
   143→          - { dir: 'environments/prod/kubernetes/platform', bucket: 'kd-tfstate-prod', project: 'kd-ix-eur-prod-gke' }
   144→      fail-fast: false
   145→    uses: ./.github/workflows/terraform-drift.yml
   146→    secrets: inherit
   147→    with:
   148→      working_directory: ${{ matrix.scope.dir }}
   149→      environment: 'prod'
   150→      gcp_project_id: ${{ matrix.scope.project }}
   151→      backend_bucket: ${{ matrix.scope.bucket }}
   152→      backend_prefix: ${{ matrix.scope.dir }}/terraform.tfstate
   153→      notify_on_drift: true
   154→      notification_channel: 'github'
   155→
   156→  drift-detection-shared:
   157→    name: Drift Detection (Shared)
   158→    if: ${{ !inputs.skip_drift }}
   159→    strategy:
   160→      matrix:
   161→        scope:
   162→          - { dir: 'environments/shared/gcp/network', bucket: 'kd-tfstate-shared', project: 'kd-ix-eur-shr-network' }
   163→          - { dir: 'environments/shared/gcp/security', bucket: 'kd-tfstate-shared', project: 'kd-ix-eur-shr-security' }
   164→          - { dir: 'environments/shared/gcp/gke', bucket: 'kd-tfstate-shared', project: 'kd-ix-eur-shr-gke' }
   165→          - { dir: 'environments/shared/kubernetes/platform', bucket: 'kd-tfstate-shared', project: 'kd-ix-eur-shr-gke' }
   166→      fail-fast: false
   167→    uses: ./.github/workflows/terraform-drift.yml
   168→    secrets: inherit
   169→    with:
   170→      working_directory: ${{ matrix.scope.dir }}
   171→      environment: 'shared'
   172→      gcp_project_id: ${{ matrix.scope.project }}
   173→      backend_bucket: ${{ matrix.scope.bucket }}
   174→      backend_prefix: ${{ matrix.scope.dir }}/terraform.tfstate
   175→      notify_on_drift: true
   176→      notification_channel: 'github'
   177→
   178→  summary:
   179→    name: Daily Quality Report
   180→    needs: [validate-develop-dev, validate-main-qa, validate-main-shared, drift-detection-dev, drift-detection-qa, drift-detection-prod, drift-detection-shared]
   181→    if: always()
   182→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
   183→    steps:
   184→      - name: Generate Report
   185→        run: |
   186→          echo "## Daily Quality Report" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   187→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   188→          echo "**Date:** $(date -u +"%Y-%m-%d")" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   189→          echo "**Time:** $(date -u +"%H:%M:%S UTC")" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   190→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   191→
   192→          echo "### Branch Validation" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   193→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   194→          if [ "${{ inputs.skip_validation }}" == "true" ]; then
   195→            echo "⏭️ Skipped (manual run with skip_validation=true)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   196→          else
   197→            # develop → dev
   198→            if [ "${{ needs.validate-develop-dev.result }}" == "success" ]; then
   199→              echo "- **develop → dev:** ✅ Validated successfully" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   200→            elif [ "${{ needs.validate-develop-dev.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
   201→              echo "- **develop → dev:** ❌ Validation failed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   202→            else
   203→              echo "- **develop → dev:** ⏭️ Skipped" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   204→            fi
   205→
   206→            # main → qa
   207→            if [ "${{ needs.validate-main-qa.result }}" == "success" ]; then
   208→              echo "- **main → qa:** ✅ Validated successfully" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   209→            elif [ "${{ needs.validate-main-qa.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
   210→              echo "- **main → qa:** ❌ Validation failed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   211→            else
   212→              echo "- **main → qa:** ⏭️ Skipped" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   213→            fi
   214→
   215→            # main → shared
   216→            if [ "${{ needs.validate-main-shared.result }}" == "success" ]; then
   217→              echo "- **main → shared:** ✅ Validated successfully" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   218→            elif [ "${{ needs.validate-main-shared.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
   219→              echo "- **main → shared:** ❌ Validation failed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   220→            else
   221→              echo "- **main → shared:** ⏭️ Skipped" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   222→            fi
   223→          fi
   224→
   225→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   226→          echo "### Drift Detection" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   227→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   228→
   229→          if [ "${{ inputs.skip_drift }}" == "true" ]; then
   230→            echo "⏭️ Skipped (manual run with skip_drift=true)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   231→          else
   232→            # Dev
   233→            if [ "${{ needs.drift-detection-dev.result }}" == "success" ]; then
   234→              echo "- **Dev:** ✅ No drift" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   235→            elif [ "${{ needs.drift-detection-dev.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
   236→              echo "- **Dev:** ⚠️ Drift detected (issues created)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   237→            else
   238→              echo "- **Dev:** ⏭️ Skipped" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   239→            fi
   240→
   241→            # QA
   242→            if [ "${{ needs.drift-detection-qa.result }}" == "success" ]; then
   243→              echo "- **QA:** ✅ No drift" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   244→            elif [ "${{ needs.drift-detection-qa.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
   245→              echo "- **QA:** ⚠️ Drift detected (issues created)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   246→            else
   247→              echo "- **QA:** ⏭️ Skipped" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   248→            fi
   249→
   250→            # Prod
   251→            if [ "${{ needs.drift-detection-prod.result }}" == "success" ]; then
   252→              echo "- **Prod:** ✅ No drift" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   253→            elif [ "${{ needs.drift-detection-prod.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
   254→              echo "- **Prod:** ⚠️ Drift detected (issues created)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   255→            else
   256→              echo "- **Prod:** ⏭️ Skipped" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   257→            fi
   258→
   259→            # Shared
   260→            if [ "${{ needs.drift-detection-shared.result }}" == "success" ]; then
   261→              echo "- **Shared:** ✅ No drift" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   262→            elif [ "${{ needs.drift-detection-shared.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
   263→              echo "- **Shared:** ⚠️ Drift detected (issues created)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   264→            else
   265→              echo "- **Shared:** ⏭️ Skipped" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   266→            fi
   267→          fi
   268→
   269→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   270→          echo "---" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   271→          echo "### Action Items" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   272→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   273→
   274→          # Check for issues
   275→          ISSUES_FOUND=false
   276→
   277→          if [ "${{ needs.validate-develop-dev.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
   278→            echo "- [ ] Investigate develop → dev validation failures" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   279→            ISSUES_FOUND=true
   280→          fi
   281→
   282→          if [ "${{ needs.validate-main-qa.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
   283→            echo "- [ ] Investigate main → qa validation failures" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   284→            ISSUES_FOUND=true
   285→          fi
   286→
   287→          if [ "${{ needs.validate-main-shared.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
   288→            echo "- [ ] Investigate main → shared validation failures" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   289→            ISSUES_FOUND=true
   290→          fi
   291→
   292→          if [ "${{ needs.drift-detection-dev.result }}" == "failure" ] || \
   293→             [ "${{ needs.drift-detection-qa.result }}" == "failure" ] || \
   294→             [ "${{ needs.drift-detection-prod.result }}" == "failure" ] || \
   295→             [ "${{ needs.drift-detection-shared.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
   296→            echo "- [ ] Review drift detection issues (check Issues tab)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   297→            ISSUES_FOUND=true
   298→          fi
   299→
   300→          if [ "$ISSUES_FOUND" == "false" ]; then
   301→            echo "✅ No action items - all checks passed!" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   302→          fi
   303→
   304→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   305→          echo "---" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   306→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   307→          echo "**Purpose:** This workflow validates that key branch-environment combinations" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   308→          echo "stay healthy and detects infrastructure drift across all environments." >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   309→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   310→          echo "*Next scheduled run: Tomorrow at 02:00 UTC*" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   311→

    54→        description: 'GitHub App ID for accessing private modules'

    66→jobs:
    67→  check-branch:
    68→    name: Check Branch and Environment
    69→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    70→    steps:
    71→      - name: Validate branch-environment combination
    72→        run: |
    73→          BRANCH="${{ github.ref_name }}"
    74→          ENV="${{ inputs.environment }}"
    75→
    76→          # Define allowed branch patterns
    77→          if [[ "$BRANCH" =~ ^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$ ]]; then
    78→            BRANCH_TYPE="tag"
    79→          elif [[ "$BRANCH" == "main" ]]; then
    80→            BRANCH_TYPE="main"
    81→          elif [[ "$BRANCH" == "develop" ]]; then
    82→            BRANCH_TYPE="develop"
    83→          else
    84→            echo "::error::Deployments not allowed from branch: $BRANCH"
    85→            echo "::error::Allowed branches: main, develop, v* tags"
    86→            exit 1
    87→          fi
    88→
    89→          # Validate branch-environment combination
    90→          case "$BRANCH_TYPE" in
    91→            tag)
    92→              # Version tags can ONLY deploy to prod
    93→              if [[ "$ENV" != "prod" ]]; then
    94→                echo "::error::Version tags (v*) can only deploy to production"
    95→                echo "::error::Current: $BRANCH → $ENV"
    96→                echo "::error::Allowed: v* → prod"
    97→                exit 1
    98→              fi
    99→              ;;
   100→            main)
   101→              # Main branch can deploy to dev, qa, bootstrap, shared (NOT prod)
   102→              if [[ "$ENV" != "dev" && "$ENV" != "qa" && "$ENV" != "bootstrap" && "$ENV" != "shared" ]]; then
   103→                echo "::error::Main branch cannot deploy to: $ENV"
   104→                echo "::error::Current: $BRANCH → $ENV"
   105→                echo "::error::Allowed: main → dev, qa, bootstrap, shared"
   106→                echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   107→                echo "To deploy to production:" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   108→                echo "1. Create a version tag: git tag -a v1.2.3 -m 'Release 1.2.3'" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   109→                echo "2. Push the tag: git push origin v1.2.3" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   110→                echo "3. Run this workflow from the v1.2.3 tag" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   111→                exit 1
   112→              fi
   113→              ;;
   114→            develop)
   115→              # Develop branch can ONLY deploy to dev environment
   116→              if [[ "$ENV" != "dev" ]]; then
   117→                echo "::error::Develop branch can only deploy to dev environment"
   118→                echo "::error::Current: $BRANCH → $ENV"
   119→                echo "::error::Allowed: develop → dev"
   120→                exit 1
   121→              fi
   122→              ;;
   123→          esac
   124→
   125→          echo "✅ Branch-environment check passed: $BRANCH → $ENV"
   126→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   127→          echo "## 🔒 Access Control" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   128→          echo "- **Branch:** $BRANCH ($BRANCH_TYPE)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   129→          echo "- **Environment:** $ENV ✅" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   130→          echo "- **Status:** Authorized" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   131→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   132→
   133→  pre-flight:
   134→    name: Pre-flight Checks
   135→    needs: check-branch
   136→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
   137→    outputs:
   138→      working_directory: ${{ steps.directory-mapping.outputs.working_directory }}
   139→      gcp_project_id: ${{ steps.directory-mapping.outputs.gcp_project_id }}
   140→      backend_bucket: ${{ steps.directory-mapping.outputs.backend_bucket }}
   141→      backend_prefix: ${{ steps.directory-mapping.outputs.backend_prefix }}
   142→      continue: ${{ steps.checks.outputs.continue }}
   143→
   144→    steps:
   145→      - name: Checkout code
   146→        uses: actions/checkout@v4
   147→
   148→      - name: Validate deployment request
   149→        run: |
   150→          echo "## Deployment Request" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   151→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   152→          echo "- **Environment:** ${{ inputs.environment }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   153→          echo "- **Scope:** ${{ inputs.scope }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   154→          echo "- **Triggered by:** @${{ github.actor }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   155→          echo "- **Branch:** ${{ github.ref_name }} ✅" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   156→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   157→          echo "> **Note:** Branch-environment access control enforced (see Access Control section above)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   158→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   159→
   160→      - name: Validate environment-scope combination
   161→        run: |
   162→          ENV="${{ inputs.environment }}"
   163→          SCOPE="${{ inputs.scope }}"
   164→
   165→          # Bootstrap environment must use bootstrap scope
   166→          if [ "$ENV" == "bootstrap" ] && [ "$SCOPE" != "bootstrap" ]; then
   167→            echo "::error::Bootstrap environment only supports 'bootstrap' scope"
   168→            echo "::error::Current: environment=$ENV, scope=$SCOPE"
   169→            echo "::error::Use scope=bootstrap when deploying to bootstrap environment"
   170→            exit 1
   171→          fi
   172→
   173→          # Other environments cannot use bootstrap scope
   174→          if [ "$ENV" != "bootstrap" ] && [ "$SCOPE" == "bootstrap" ]; then
   175→            echo "::error::Bootstrap scope can only be used with bootstrap environment"
   176→            echo "::error::Current: environment=$ENV, scope=$SCOPE"
   177→            echo "::error::Bootstrap scope is reserved for the bootstrap environment"
   178→            exit 1
   179→          fi
   180→
   181→          echo "✅ Environment-scope combination valid: $ENV → $SCOPE"
   182→
   183→      - name: Check change window (Production only)
   184→        if: inputs.environment == 'prod'
   185→        run: |
   186→          # Get current UTC time
   187→          HOUR=$(date -u +%H)
   188→          DAY=$(date -u +%u)  # 1=Monday, 7=Sunday
   189→
   190→          echo "**Change Window Check**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   191→          echo "- Current UTC time: $(date -u)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   192→          echo "- Day of week: $(date -u +%A)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   193→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   194→
   195→          # Prod changes only allowed Tue-Thu 20:00-23:00 UTC
   196→          if [[ $DAY -lt 2 || $DAY -gt 4 ]]; then
   197→            echo "::error::Production deployments only allowed Tuesday-Thursday"
   198→            echo "❌ **Outside change window:** Production deployments only allowed Tuesday-Thursday" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   199→            exit 1
   200→          fi
   201→
   202→          if [[ $HOUR -lt 20 || $HOUR -gt 23 ]]; then
   203→            echo "::error::Production deployments only allowed 20:00-23:00 UTC"
   204→            echo "❌ **Outside change window:** Production deployments only allowed 20:00-23:00 UTC" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   205→            exit 1
   206→          fi
   207→
   208→          echo "✅ **Within approved change window**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   209→
   210→      - name: Map scope to directory
   211→        id: directory-mapping
   212→        run: |
   213→          ENV="${{ inputs.environment }}"
   214→          SCOPE="${{ inputs.scope }}"
   215→
   216→          # Determine working directory based on environment and scope
   217→          if [ "$SCOPE" == "bootstrap" ]; then
   218→            WORK_DIR="environments/bootstrap"
   219→            PROJECT_ID="kd-ix-eur-shr-bootstrap"
   220→            BUCKET="kd-tfstate-bootstrap"
   221→          elif [ "$SCOPE" == "network" ]; then
   222→            WORK_DIR="environments/$ENV/gcp/network"
   223→            PROJECT_ID="kd-ix-eur-$ENV-network"
   224→            BUCKET="kd-tfstate-$ENV"
   225→          elif [ "$SCOPE" == "security" ]; then
   226→            WORK_DIR="environments/$ENV/gcp/security"
   227→            PROJECT_ID="kd-ix-eur-$ENV-security"
   228→            BUCKET="kd-tfstate-$ENV"
   229→          elif [ "$SCOPE" == "gke" ]; then
   230→            WORK_DIR="environments/$ENV/gcp/gke"
   231→            PROJECT_ID="kd-ix-eur-$ENV-gke"
   232→            BUCKET="kd-tfstate-$ENV"
   233→          elif [ "$SCOPE" == "data" ]; then
   234→            WORK_DIR="environments/$ENV/gcp/data"
   235→            PROJECT_ID="kd-ix-eur-$ENV-platform-data"
   236→            BUCKET="kd-tfstate-$ENV"
   237→          elif [ "$SCOPE" == "iam" ]; then
   238→            WORK_DIR="environments/$ENV/gcp/iam"
   239→            PROJECT_ID="kd-ix-eur-$ENV-gke"
   240→            BUCKET="kd-tfstate-$ENV"
   241→          elif [[ "$SCOPE" == "platform" || "$SCOPE" == "kong" || "$SCOPE" == "gateway" || "$SCOPE" == "litellm" || "$SCOPE" == "argocd" ]]; then
   242→            WORK_DIR="environments/$ENV/kubernetes/$SCOPE"
   243→            PROJECT_ID="kd-ix-eur-$ENV-gke"
   244→            BUCKET="kd-tfstate-$ENV"
   245→          else
   246→            echo "::error::Unknown scope: $SCOPE"
   247→            exit 1
   248→          fi
   249→
   250→          # Compute backend prefix by stripping "environments/{env}/" prefix
   251→          # This matches the backend.tf convention: "gcp/network" not "environments/dev/gcp/network"
   252→          BACKEND_PREFIX=$(echo "$WORK_DIR" | sed -E 's#^environments/(dev|qa|prod|shared|lab)/##; s#^environments/##')
   253→
   254→          echo "working_directory=$WORK_DIR" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   255→          echo "gcp_project_id=$PROJECT_ID" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   256→          echo "backend_bucket=$BUCKET" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   257→          echo "backend_prefix=$BACKEND_PREFIX" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   258→
   259→          echo "**Deployment Target**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   260→          echo "- Directory: \`$WORK_DIR\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   261→          echo "- GCP Project: \`$PROJECT_ID\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   262→          echo "- State Bucket: \`gs://$BUCKET\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   263→          echo "- Backend Prefix: \`$BACKEND_PREFIX\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   264→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   265→
   266→      - name: Checks complete
   267→        id: checks
   268→        run: echo "continue=true" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   269→
   270→  validate:
   271→    name: Validate Terraform
   272→    needs: pre-flight
   273→    uses: ./.github/workflows/terraform-validate.yml
   274→    secrets: inherit
   275→    with:
   276→      working_directory: ${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.working_directory }}
   277→      terraform_version: '1.7.4'
   278→      enable_tfsec: true
   279→      tfsec_severity: 'MEDIUM'
   280→      ref: ${{ inputs.ref }}
   281→
   282→  plan:
   283→    name: Plan Changes
   284→    needs: [pre-flight, validate]
   285→    uses: ./.github/workflows/terraform-plan.yml
   286→    secrets: inherit
   287→    with:
   288→      working_directory: ${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.working_directory }}
   289→      terraform_version: '1.7.4'
   290→      environment: ${{ inputs.environment }}
   291→      gcp_project_id: ${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.gcp_project_id }}
   292→      backend_bucket: ${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.backend_bucket }}
   293→      # Backend prefix computed in pre-flight job (stripped "environments/{env}/" to match backend.tf)
   294→      backend_prefix: ${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.backend_prefix }}
   295→      comment_on_pr: false
   296→      upload_artifact: true
   297→      ref: ${{ inputs.ref }}
   298→
   299→  summary:
   300→    name: Validation Summary
   301→    needs: [pre-flight, validate, plan]
   302→    if: always()
   303→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
   304→    steps:
   305→      - name: Generate Summary
   306→        run: |
   307→          echo "## 📋 Terraform Validation and Plan Summary" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   308→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   309→
   310→          # Overall Status
   311→          OVERALL_STATUS="✅ Validation and Plan Complete"
   312→          if [ "${{ needs.validate.result }}" == "failure" ] || [ "${{ needs.plan.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
   313→            OVERALL_STATUS="❌ Failed"
   314→          fi
   315→
   316→          echo "### Overall Status: $OVERALL_STATUS" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   317→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   318→
   319→          # Details
   320→          echo "**Environment:** ${{ inputs.environment }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   321→          echo "**Scope:** ${{ inputs.scope }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   322→          echo "**Directory:** \`${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.working_directory }}\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   323→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   324→
   325→          # Plan Changes (from plan job outputs)
   326→          ADD="${{ needs.plan.outputs.add_count || '0' }}"
   327→          CHANGE="${{ needs.plan.outputs.change_count || '0' }}"
   328→          DESTROY="${{ needs.plan.outputs.destroy_count || '0' }}"
   329→
   330→          echo "**Planned Changes:**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   331→          if [ "$ADD" != "0" ] || [ "$CHANGE" != "0" ] || [ "$DESTROY" != "0" ]; then
   332→            echo "- $ADD to add, $CHANGE to change, $DESTROY to destroy" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   333→          else
   334→            echo "- No infrastructure changes" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   335→          fi
   336→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   337→
   338→          # Job Results
   339→          echo "**Job Results:**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   340→          if [ "${{ needs.validate.result }}" == "success" ]; then
   341→            echo "- ✅ Validation: Passed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   342→          else
   343→            echo "- ❌ Validation: Failed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   344→          fi
   345→
   346→          if [ "${{ needs.plan.result }}" == "success" ]; then
   347→            echo "- ✅ Plan: Generated successfully" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   348→          else
   349→            echo "- ❌ Plan: Failed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   350→          fi
   351→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   352→
   353→          echo "---" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   354→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   355→
   356→          # Next Steps
   357→          echo "### Next Steps" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   358→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   359→
   360→          if [ "${{ needs.validate.result }}" == "failure" ] || [ "${{ needs.plan.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
   361→            echo "❌ **Validation or planning failed** - Review error logs above" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   362→          else
   363→            echo "✅ **Validation and plan successful**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   364→            echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   365→            echo "To apply these changes, use the **'Validate, Plan and Apply'** workflow." >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   366→          fi
   367→
   368→      - name: Check Overall Status
   369→        if: needs.validate.result == 'failure' || needs.plan.result == 'failure'
   370→        run: |
   371→          echo "::error::Validation or planning failed. Check the job logs for details."
   372→          exit 1

\"${{ needs.plan.result }}\" == \"failure\" ]; then\n            OVERALL_STATUS=\"Failed\"\n          fi\n\n          echo \"### Overall Status: $OVERALL_STATUS\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n\n          echo \"**Environment:** ${{ inputs.environment }}\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"**Directory:** \\`${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.working_directory }}\\`\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n\n          ADD=\"${{ needs.plan.outputs.add_count || '0' }}\"\n          CHANGE=\"${{ needs.plan.outputs.change_count || '0' }}\"\n          DESTROY=\"${{ needs.plan.outputs.destroy_count || '0' }}\"\n\n          echo \"**Planned Changes:**\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          if [ \"$ADD\" != \"0\" ] || [ \"$CHANGE\" != \"0\" ] || [ \"$DESTROY\" != \"0\" ]; then\n            echo \"- $ADD to add, $CHANGE to change, $DESTROY to destroy\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          else\n            echo \"- No infrastructure changes\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          fi\n          echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n\n          echo \"**Job Results:**\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          if [ \"${{ needs.validate.result }}\" == \"success\" ]; then\n            echo \"- Validation: Passed\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          else\n            echo \"- Validation: Failed\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          fi\n\n          if [ \"${{ needs.plan.result }}\" == \"success\" ]; then\n            echo \"- Plan: Generated successfully\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          else\n            echo \"- Plan: Failed\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          fi\n\n      - name: Check Overall Status\n        if: needs.validate.result == 'failure' || needs.plan.result == 'failure'\n        run: |\n          echo \"::error::Validation or planning failed. Check the job logs for details.\"\n          exit 1\n"
}

   236→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY

   238→          ADD="${{ needs.plan.outputs.add_count || '0' }}"
   239→          CHANGE="${{ needs.plan.outputs.change_count || '0' }}"
   240→          DESTROY="${{ needs.plan.outputs.destroy_count || '0' }}"
   241→
   242→          echo "**Changes Applied:**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   243→          if [ "$ADD" != "0" ] || [ "$CHANGE" != "0" ] || [ "$DESTROY" != "0" ]; then
   244→            echo "- $ADD to add, $CHANGE to change, $DESTROY to destroy" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   245→          else
   246→            echo "- No infrastructure changes" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   247→          fi
   248→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   249→
   250→          echo "**Job Results:**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   251→          for JOB in validate plan review-gate apply; do
   252→            RESULT="${{ needs[JOB].result || 'unknown' }}"
   253→            case "$RESULT" in
   254→              success) echo "- $JOB: Passed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY ;;
   255→              failure) echo "- $JOB: Failed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY ;;
   256→              skipped) echo "- $JOB: Skipped" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY ;;
   257→              *) echo "- $JOB: $RESULT" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY ;;
   258→            esac
   259→          done
   260→
   261→      - name: Check Overall Status
   262→        if: needs.apply.result == 'failure'
   263→        run: |

   265→          exit 1

    64→          PROJECT_ID="kd-ix-eur-$ENV-gke"
    65→          BUCKET="kd-tfstate-$ENV"
    66→
    67→          if [ ! -d "$WORK_DIR" ]; then
    68→            echo "::error::Terraform directory not found: $WORK_DIR"
    69→            exit 1
    70→          fi
    71→
    72→          echo "working_directory=$WORK_DIR" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
    73→          echo "gcp_project_id=$PROJECT_ID" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
    74→          echo "backend_bucket=$BUCKET" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
    75→
    76→          echo "**Validation Target**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
    77→          echo "- Environment: \`$ENV\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
    78→          echo "- Directory: \`$WORK_DIR\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
    79→          echo "- GCP Project: \`$PROJECT_ID\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
    80→          echo "- State Bucket: \`gs://$BUCKET\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
    81→          echo "- Backend Prefix: \`services/crewai/resources\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
    82→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
    83→
    84→  validate:
    85→    name: Validate Terraform
    86→    needs: pre-flight
    87→    uses: ./.github/workflows/terraform-validate.yml
    88→    secrets: inherit
    89→    with:
    90→      working_directory: ${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.working_directory }}
    91→      terraform_version: '1.5.0'
    92→      enable_tfsec: true
    93→      tfsec_severity: 'MEDIUM'
    94→      ref: ${{ inputs.ref }}
    95→
    96→  plan:
    97→    name: Plan Changes
    98→    needs: [pre-flight, validate]
    99→    uses: ./.github/workflows/terraform-plan.yml
   100→    secrets: inherit
   101→    with:
   102→      working_directory: ${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.working_directory }}
   103→      terraform_version: '1.5.0'
   104→      environment: ${{ inputs.environment }}
   105→      gcp_project_id: ${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.gcp_project_id }}
   106→      backend_bucket: ${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.backend_bucket }}
   107→      backend_prefix: services/crewai/resources
   108→      comment_on_pr: false
   109→      upload_artifact: true
   110→      ref: ${{ inputs.ref }}
   111→
   112→  summary:
   113→    name: Validation Summary
   114→    needs: [pre-flight, validate, plan]
   115→    if: always()
   116→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
   117→    steps:
   118→      - name: Generate Summary
   119→        run: |
   120→          echo "## Terraform Validation and Plan Summary" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   121→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   122→
   123→          OVERALL_STATUS="Validation and Plan Complete"
   124→          if [ "${{ needs.validate.result }}" == "failure" ] || [ "${{ needs.plan.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
   125→            OVERALL_STATUS="Failed"
   126→          fi
   127→
   128→          echo "### Overall Status: $OVERALL_STATUS" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   129→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   130→
   131→          echo "**Environment:** ${{ inputs.environment }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   132→          echo "**Directory:** \`${{ needs.pre-flight.outputs.working_directory }}\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   133→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   134→
   135→          ADD="${{ needs.plan.outputs.add_count || '0' }}"
   136→          CHANGE="${{ needs.plan.outputs.change_count || '0' }}"
   137→          DESTROY="${{ needs.plan.outputs.destroy_count || '0' }}"
   138→
   139→          echo "**Planned Changes:**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   140→          if [ "$ADD" != "0" ] || [ "$CHANGE" != "0" ] || [ "$DESTROY" != "0" ]; then
   141→            echo "- $ADD to add, $CHANGE to change, $DESTROY to destroy" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   142→          else
   143→            echo "- No infrastructure changes" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   144→          fi
   145→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   146→
   147→          echo "**Job Results:**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   148→          if [ "${{ needs.validate.result }}" == "success" ]; then
   149→            echo "- Validation: Passed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   150→          else
   151→            echo "- Validation: Failed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   152→          fi
   153→
   154→          if [ "${{ needs.plan.result }}" == "success" ]; then
   155→            echo "- Plan: Generated successfully" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   156→          else
   157→            echo "- Plan: Failed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   158→          fi
   159→
   160→      - name: Check Overall Status
   161→        if: needs.validate.result == 'failure' || needs.plan.result == 'failure'
   162→        run: |
   163→          echo "::error::Validation or planning failed. Check the job logs for details."
   164→          exit 1
   165→

> AGENT

All done. Here's a summary of what was changed:

**Modified workflows (6 files):**
- **terraform-plan.yml** - Replaced platform auth case statement with `secrets.GCP_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER` / `secrets.GCP_SERVICE_ACCOUNT`
- **terraform-apply.yml** - Same auth simplification
- **terraform-drift.yml** - Same auth simplification + fixed terraform version 1.7.4 → 1.5.0
- **validate-plan-apply.yml** - Major rewrite: removed scope/bootstrap/shared concepts, simplified to `environments/{env}/terraform/` with static backend prefix `services/crewai/resources`
- **manual-validate-plan.yml** - Same major rewrite, removed scope input and platform directory mapping
- **nightly-quality.yml** - Simplified to dev/qa/prod only, removed shared env, scope matrices, and platform directory paths

**Deleted workflows (2 files):**
- **terraform-test.yml** - No test directory in this repo
- **weekly-quality.yml** - Referenced automated-testing/lab environments that don't exist

Net result: -1,256 lines removed across all files, keeping only what's relevant to this repo's `environments/{dev,qa,prod}/terraform/` structure.

> AGENT

It depends on the trigger type:

- **`schedule`** (cron) - Only runs from the **default branch** (usually `main`)
- **`workflow_dispatch`** (manual) - Must exist on the **default branch** to appear in the Actions UI. You can trigger it on other branches via API, but the UI dropdown only shows branches where the workflow exists on the default branch.
- **`pull_request`** - Uses the workflow from the **PR's head branch**, so it works from feature branches
- **`push`** - Uses the workflow from the branch being pushed to

For your workflows specifically:
- `validate-and-plan.yml` (PR trigger) - will work from the feature branch
- `validate-plan-apply.yml` and `manual-validate-plan.yml` (workflow_dispatch) - need to be on `main`/default branch to show in the UI
- `nightly-quality.yml` (schedule) - will only run from `main`

So yes, you'll need to merge these to your default branch for the dispatch and scheduled workflows to be functional.

This is expected behavior. The PR only contains workflow file changes (`.yml` files) - no terraform files (`.tf`) were modified. The change detection correctly found no terraform changes, so Validate and Plan were skipped.

The workflow trigger fires on `.github/workflows/**` paths, but the detect-changes job specifically looks for `environments/*/terraform/**/*.tf` file changes to build the validation matrix. Since there are none, `has_changes=false` and the downstream jobs skip.

This is correct - there's no terraform to validate or plan in this PR.

> AGENT

The `helm_registry_token` variable is used in all three environments and needs to be provided when running `terraform apply`. There are two places you need to define it:

**1. GCP Secret Manager** (managed by Terraform itself)
The terraform creates a secret `crewai-helm-registry-token-{env}` in GCP Secret Manager via the `secret_helm_registry` module. This stores the token for runtime use.

**2. Terraform variable input** (needed at plan/apply time)
The `helm_registry_token` variable in [variables.tf:52](environments/dev/terraform/variables.tf#L52) is marked sensitive and has no default. You need to supply it via one of:

- **GitHub Actions secret** - Add `TF_VAR_helm_registry_token` as a repository secret (or per-environment secret). Terraform automatically picks up `TF_VAR_*` environment variables. You'd need to add this to the terraform-plan and terraform-apply workflows:
  ```yaml
  env:
    TF_VAR_helm_registry_token: ${{ secrets.CREWAI_HELM_REGISTRY_TOKEN }}
  ```

- **`terraform.tfvars` file** (not committed) - locally or via CI

Right now, the workflows don't pass this variable, so `terraform plan` and `terraform apply` will prompt for it (and fail in CI). You'll need to:
1. Add `CREWAI_HELM_REGISTRY_TOKEN` as a GitHub repository secret (or per-environment secret)
2. Add the `env` block to the terraform-plan and terraform-apply reusable workflows

Want me to add the `TF_VAR_` environment variable to the workflows?

```
     1→# CrewAI Platform - GCP Deployment
     2→
     3→Multi-environment deployment infrastructure for CrewAI Platform on Google Kubernetes Engine (GKE). 
     4→
     5→## Overview
     6→
     7→This repository contains Infrastructure as Code (Terraform) and GitOps (ArgoCD) configurations for deploying CrewAI Platform across three environments:
     8→
     9→- **Development**: `crewai.dev.ix.konecta-digital.com` (kd-ix-eur-dev-gke)
    10→- **QA**: `crewai.qa.ix.konecta-digital.com` (kd-ix-eur-qa-gke)
    11→- **Production**: `crewai.ix.konecta-digital.com` (kd-ix-eur-prod-gke)
    12→
    13→## Repository Structure
    14→
    15→```
    16→kix-services-crewai/
    17→├── environments/
    18→│   ├── dev/
    19→│   │   ├── argocd/
    20→│   │   │   └── application.yaml      # ArgoCD Application for dev
    21→│   │   ├── helm/
    22→│   │   │   └── values.yaml           # Helm values for OCI chart
    23→│   │   └── terraform/
    24→│   │       ├── backend.tf            # GCS state backend (kd-tfstate-dev)
    25→│   │       ├── main.tf               # Infrastructure + Secrets + IAM
    26→│   │       ├── outputs.tf
    27→│   │       ├── variables.tf
    28→│   │       ├── versions.tf
    29→│   │       └── README.md             # Auto-generated terraform-docs
    30→│   ├── qa/
    31→│   │   ├── argocd/application.yaml
    32→│   │   ├── helm/values.yaml
    33→│   │   └── terraform/               # Same structure as dev
    34→│   └── prod/
    35→│       ├── argocd/application.yaml
    36→│       ├── helm/values.yaml
    37→│       └── terraform/               # Same structure as dev
    38→│
    39→└── docs/
    40→    ├── prps/                         # Product Requirements & Plans
    41→    └── tasks/                        # Task breakdown documents
    42→```
    43→
    44→## Architecture
    45→
    46→- **Infrastructure**: Terraform per environment composing remote modules from [kix-platform-modules](https://github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules)
    47→- **Deployment**: External OCI Helm chart (`oci://registry.crewai.com/crewai/stable`) deployed via ArgoCD
    48→- **CI/CD**: GitHub Actions for automated testing, security scanning, and progressive deployment
    49→- **Secrets**: Terraform creates secrets in GCP Secret Manager and K8s secrets for application consumption
    50→- **Database**: Cloud SQL databases on existing PostgreSQL instances with Cloud SQL Proxy sidecar
    51→- **Storage**: GCS buckets (data + logs) with S3-compatible HMAC access
    52→- **Service Accounts**: GCP service account with Workload Identity binding to K8s service account
    53→- **Namespaces**: Dual namespaces per environment (`crewai-{env}` + `crewai-crews-{env}`) with resource quotas and network policies
    54→
    55→## Prerequisites
    56→
    57→### Required Tools
    58→
    59→- Terraform >= 1.5.0
    60→- kubectl >= 1.28
    61→- gcloud CLI
    62→- helm >= 3.12
    63→- argocd CLI (optional)
    64→
    65→### GCP Resources (Pre-existing)
    66→
    67→| Environment | GKE Cluster | Cloud SQL Instance | Project |
    68→|------------|-------------|-------------------|---------|
    69→| Dev | kd-ix-eur-dev-gke | kd-ix-eur-dev-cloudsql-platform | kd-ix-eur-dev-platform-data |
    70→| QA | kd-ix-eur-qa-gke | kd-ix-eur-qa-cloudsql-platform | kd-ix-eur-qa-platform-data |
    71→| Prod | kd-ix-eur-prod-gke | kd-ix-eur-prod-cloudsql-platform | kd-ix-eur-prod-platform-data |
    72→
    73→## CI/CD Pipeline
    74→
    75→This repository uses **GitHub Actions** for continuous integration and deployment with GitOps practices.
    76→
    77→### Automated Workflows
    78→
    79→1. **Terraform Plan** - Validates infrastructure changes on PRs
    80→   - Runs on: Pull requests modifying `terraform/**`
    81→   - Outputs: Terraform plan, security scan results
    82→
    83→2. **Terraform Apply** - Deploys infrastructure changes
    84→   - Runs on: Push to `main` branch
    85→   - Progression: Dev → QA → Production (with approvals)
    86→
    87→3. **ArgoCD Sync** - Deploys application via GitOps
    88→   - Runs on: Push to `main` branch (helm changes)
    89→   - Progression: Dev → QA → Production (with approvals)
    90→
    91→4. **Promote Release** - Promotes tested versions between environments
    92→   - Runs on: Manual trigger
    93→   - Valid paths: Dev → QA, QA → Production
    94→
    95→5. **Health Checks** - Monitors application health
    96→   - Runs on: Schedule (every 15 minutes)
    97→
    98→### Deployment Flow
    99→
   100→```
   101→Developer → PR → Terraform Plan → Merge → Dev Deploy
   102→                                            ↓
   103→                                    Promote → QA Deploy
   104→                                                  ↓
   105→                                    Approve → Production Deploy
   106→```
   107→
   108→## Quick Start
   109→
   110→### 1. Prerequisites Setup
   111→
   112→```bash
   113→gcloud auth login
   114→gcloud auth application-default login
   115→gcloud config set project kd-ix-eur-dev-gke
   116→gcloud container clusters get-credentials kd-ix-eur-dev-gke \
   117→  --region=europe-west1 \
   118→  --project=kd-ix-eur-dev-gke
   119→```
   120→
   121→### 2. Deploy Infrastructure (Dev)
   122→
   123→```bash
   124→cd environments/dev/terraform
   125→terraform init
   126→terraform plan
   127→terraform apply
   128→```
   129→
   130→This creates:
   131→- Cloud SQL databases (production, cable, oauth) and user on the existing instance
   132→- GCS buckets for data and logs storage
   133→- GCP service account with HMAC keys for S3-compatible GCS access
   134→- Secrets in GCP Secret Manager (database, GCS, OAuth, Helm registry)
   135→- IAM bindings (Cloud SQL client, Secret Manager accessor, GCS access, Workload Identity)
   136→- Kubernetes namespaces with resource quotas and network policies
   137→- K8s secrets for application credential consumption
   138→
   139→### 3. Deploy Application via ArgoCD
   140→
   141→Apply the ArgoCD application manifest:
   142→
   143→```bash
   144→kubectl apply -f environments/dev/argocd/application.yaml -n argocd
   145→```
   146→
   147→ArgoCD will deploy the CrewAI Platform Helm chart with environment-specific values.
   148→
   149→### 4. Verify Deployment
   150→
   151→```bash
   152→kubectl get pods -n crewai-dev
   153→kubectl get svc -n crewai-dev
   154→curl https://crewai.dev.ix.konecta-digital.com/health
   155→```
   156→
   157→## Environment Configuration
   158→
   159→### Development
   160→- 1 replica for web and worker pods
   161→- CPU: 1000m requests, 2000m limits
   162→- Memory: 2Gi requests, 4Gi limits
   163→- Auto-sync enabled in ArgoCD
   164→
   165→### QA
   166→- 2 replicas for web and worker pods
   167→- CPU: 1500m requests, 3000m limits
   168→- Memory: 4Gi requests, 8Gi limits
   169→- Auto-sync enabled in ArgoCD
   170→
   171→### Production
   172→- 3+ replicas with HPA (max 10)
   173→- CPU: 2000m requests, 4000m limits
   174→- Memory: 6Gi requests, 12Gi limits
   175→- Pod Disruption Budget: min 2 available
   176→- Pod anti-affinity and topology spread
   177→- Manual sync in ArgoCD (production safety)
   178→
   179→## Terraform
   180→
   181→Each environment's Terraform composes remote modules from [kix-platform-modules](https://github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules) with inline resources:
   182→
   183→**Remote modules:**
   184→- `data-services/gcs` - GCS buckets (data + logs)
   185→- `secret-manager` - GCP Secret Manager secrets (DB, GCS, OAuth, Helm registry)
   186→- `gke-config` - Kubernetes namespaces, resource quotas, network policies, service accounts
   187→
   188→**Inline resources:**
   189→- Cloud SQL databases and user on existing instance
   190→- GCP service account (inline to avoid circular dependency with HMAC keys)
   191→- HMAC keys for S3-compatible GCS access
   192→- IAM bindings (Cloud SQL, Secret Manager, GCS, Workload Identity)
   193→- Kubernetes secrets for application credential consumption
   194→
   195→State is stored in per-environment GCS buckets (`kd-tfstate-{env}`) with prefix `services/crewai/resources`.
   196→
   197→## Maintenance
   198→
   199→### Rotating Secrets
   200→
   201→Re-run `terraform apply` in the relevant environment. Terraform manages the database password and HMAC keys - it will regenerate and update both Secret Manager and K8s secrets:
   202→
   203→```bash
   204→cd environments/dev/terraform
   205→terraform apply
   206→```
   207→
   208→### Upgrading CrewAI Version
   209→
   210→Update `targetRevision` in the environment's ArgoCD application:
   211→
   212→```bash
   213→# Edit environments/{env}/argocd/application.yaml
   214→# Change targetRevision from 0.3.10 to new version
   215→# Commit and push - ArgoCD auto-syncs (dev/qa) or manually sync (prod)
   216→```
   217→
   218→## Troubleshooting
   219→
   220→### Pods not starting
   221→
   222→```bash
   223→kubectl describe pod <pod-name> -n crewai-dev

  "old_string": "## Prerequisites\n\n### Required Tools\n\n- Terraform >= 1.5.0\n- kubectl >= 1.28\n- gcloud CLI\n- helm >= 3.12\n- argocd CLI (optional)\n\n### GCP Resources (Pre-existing)\n\n| Environment | GKE Cluster | Cloud SQL Instance | Project |\n|------------|-------------|-------------------|---------|\n| Dev | kd-ix-eur-dev-gke | kd-ix-eur-dev-cloudsql-platform | kd-ix-eur-dev-platform-data |\n| QA | kd-ix-eur-qa-gke | kd-ix-eur-qa-cloudsql-platform | kd-ix-eur-qa-platform-data |\n| Prod | kd-ix-eur-prod-gke | kd-ix-eur-prod-cloudsql-platform | kd-ix-eur-prod-platform-data |\n\n## CI/CD Pipeline\n\nThis repository uses **GitHub Actions** for continuous integration and deployment with GitOps practices.\n\n### Automated Workflows\n\n1. **Terraform Plan** - Validates infrastructure changes on PRs\n   - Runs on: Pull requests modifying `terraform/**`\n   - Outputs: Terraform plan, security scan results\n\n2. **Terraform Apply** - Deploys infrastructure changes\n   - Runs on: Push to `main` branch\n   - Progression: Dev → QA → Production (with approvals)\n\n3. **ArgoCD Sync** - Deploys application via GitOps\n   - Runs on: Push to `main` branch (helm changes)\n   - Progression: Dev → QA → Production (with approvals)\n\n4. **Promote Release** - Promotes tested versions between environments\n   - Runs on: Manual trigger\n   - Valid paths: Dev → QA, QA → Production\n\n5. **Health Checks** - Monitors application health\n   - Runs on: Schedule (every 15 minutes)\n\n### Deployment Flow\n\n```\nDeveloper → PR → Terraform Plan → Merge → Dev Deploy\n                                            ↓\n                                    Promote → QA Deploy\n                                                  ↓\n                                    Approve → Production Deploy\n```",

Secrets can be set at the **repository level** (applies to all environments) or as **environment-scoped secrets** (per GitHub Environment) for environment-specific credentials.\n\n**Settings → Secrets and variables → Actions**\n\n#### Repository Secrets (all environments)\n\n| Secret | Used By | Description |\n|--------|---------|-------------|\n| `GCP_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER` | terraform-plan, terraform-apply, terraform-drift | GCP Workload Identity Federation provider ID (e.g. `projects/PROJECT_NUMBER/locations/global/workloadIdentityPools/POOL/providers/PROVIDER`). Set up via the `github-actions-oidc` module in [kix-platform-infrastructure](https://github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-infrastructure). |\n| `GCP_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` | terraform-plan, terraform-apply, terraform-drift | GCP service account email for GitHub Actions (e.g. `github-kix-services-crewai-dev@kd-ix-eur-dev-gke.iam.gserviceaccount.com`). |\n| `TERRAFORM_MODULES_APP_ID` | terraform-plan, terraform-apply, terraform-drift, terraform-validate | GitHub App ID for accessing private Terraform modules in [kix-platform-modules](https://github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules). |\n| `TERRAFORM_MODULES_APP_PRIVATE_KEY` | terraform-plan, terraform-apply, terraform-drift, terraform-validate | GitHub App private key (PEM) for the above App. |\n\n> **Note:** If you need different GCP credentials per environment (e.g. separate workload identity pools for dev/qa/prod), configure `GCP_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER` and `GCP_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` as environment-scoped secrets on the `dev`, `qa`, and `prod` GitHub Environments instead of repository-level secrets.\n\n#### Terraform Variables (sensitive inputs)\n\nThese are passed to Terraform at plan/apply time via `TF_VAR_*` environment variables. They should be stored as GitHub Actions secrets and need to be added to the terraform-plan and terraform-apply workflows.\n\n| Secret | Terraform Variable | Description |\n|--------|--------------------|-------------|\n| `TF_VAR_oauth_client_id` | `oauth_client_id` | WorkOS OAuth client ID |\n| `TF_VAR_oauth_api_key` | `oauth_api_key` | WorkOS OAuth API key |\n| `TF_VAR_oauth_domain` | `oauth_domain` | WorkOS OAuth domain |\n| `TF_VAR_helm_registry_token` | `helm_registry_token` | CrewAI Helm OCI registry (`registry.crewai.com`) authentication token |\n\n> **Note:** These are currently **not wired** into the GitHub Actions workflows. They must be added as `env:` blocks on the Terraform Init/Plan/Apply steps, or set as environment-scoped secrets with `TF_VAR_` prefix so Terraform picks them up automatically.\n\n#### GitHub Environments (for approval gates)\n\nThe `validate-plan-apply` workflow uses GitHub Environment protection rules for deployment approvals. Create these environments in **Settings → Environments**:\n\n| Environment | Purpose |\n|-------------|---------|\n| `dev-approval` | Approval gate before applying to dev |\n| `qa-approval` | Approval gate before applying to QA |\n| `prod-approval` | Approval gate before applying to production |\n\n## CI/CD Pipeline\n\nThis repository uses **GitHub Actions** for continuous integration and deployment.\n\n### Automated Workflows\n\n| Workflow | Trigger | Description |\n|----------|---------|-------------|\n| **Auto Validate and Plan** | PR to `main`/`develop` modifying `environments/*/terraform/**` | Detects changed Terraform directories, runs validation + security scan + plan |\n| **Validate, Plan and Apply** | Manual (`workflow_dispatch`) | Full deploy pipeline: validate → plan → approval gate → apply |\n| **Validate and Plan** | Manual / called by nightly | Validate + plan for a single environment (no apply) |\n| **Nightly Quality Checks** | Schedule (02:00 UTC daily) | Validates develop→dev, main→qa and runs drift detection on all environments |\n\n### Reusable Workflows\n\n| Workflow | Description |\n|----------|-------------|\n| `terraform-validate.yml` | Terraform fmt, validate, and Trivy security scan |\n| `terraform-plan.yml` | Terraform init + plan with statistics extraction |\n| `terraform-apply.yml` | Terraform apply with pre/post validation |\n| `terraform-drift.yml` | Drift detection with GitHub issue creation |\n\n### Branch-Environment Rules\n\n| Branch | Allowed Environments |\n|--------|---------------------|\n| `develop` | dev |\n| `main` | dev, qa |\n| `v*` tags | prod |\n\n### Deployment Flow\n\n```\nDeveloper → PR → Validate + Plan → Merge to develop\n                                            ↓\n                          Manual dispatch → Dev Apply (with approval)\n                                            ↓\n                          Manual dispatch → QA Apply (with approval)\n                                            ↓\n                        Tag release v* → Prod Apply (with approval, change window enforced)\n```"
}

> DEVELOPER

you said CREWAI_HELM_REGISTRY_TOKEN  was needed?

> AGENT

Sorry for the confusion - the two names I mentioned were different approaches to the same thing. You only need one:

**Option 1 (simplest):** Name the GitHub secret `TF_VAR_helm_registry_token`. If it's exposed as an environment variable, Terraform picks it up automatically with no workflow changes needed.

**Option 2:** Name it whatever you want (e.g. `CREWAI_HELM_REGISTRY_TOKEN`) and explicitly map it in the workflow:
```yaml
env:
  TF_VAR_helm_registry_token: ${{ secrets.CREWAI_HELM_REGISTRY_TOKEN }}
```

Either way, right now **neither approach is wired up** in the workflows. The `TF_VAR_*` secrets aren't being passed as environment variables to the Terraform steps, so `terraform plan` will fail when it hits the required `helm_registry_token` variable with no value.

The same applies to `oauth_client_id`, `oauth_api_key`, and `oauth_domain` - all four are required sensitive variables with no defaults.

> AGENT

It isn't defined separately. The `dockerconfigjson` auth field in the Kubernetes secret (e.g. [main.tf:364](environments/dev/terraform/main.tf#L364)) is set directly to `var.helm_registry_token` - it's using the token as a pre-encoded `auth` value, not splitting into `username` and `password`.

In Docker's `config.json` format, the `auth` field is a base64-encoded `username:password` string. So the `helm_registry_token` variable is expected to already contain that base64 value (e.g. `base64("myuser:mypassword")`).

There's no separate username variable - it's baked into the token value you provide.

> AGENT

That means ArgoCD isn't installed on the target GKE cluster (or at least its CRDs aren't). The `Application` custom resource requires ArgoCD to be deployed first.

Is ArgoCD managed by the platform team on these clusters, or does it need to be set up for this project? If it's supposed to be there already, check with the platform team - it may not be installed on the dev cluster yet.

If you want to skip ArgoCD for now and deploy the Helm chart directly instead:

```bash
helm registry login registry.crewai.com
helm install crewai-dev oci://registry.crewai.com/crewai/stable \
  --version 0.3.10 \
  --namespace crewai-dev \
  -f environments/dev/helm/values.yaml
```
